Notably, our model significantly outperforms the concurrent Qwen-Image-Layered model in image-to-layers quality according to user-study results, while achieving 10-100\times faster inference and reducing activation GPU memory consumption by 50-90\% during image-to-layer inference.

</details>


### [113] [Can Retrieval Heads See Images? Multimodal Retrieval Heads in Long-Context Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27243)
*Aaron Branson Cigres Li,Zhaowei Wang,Yu Zhao,Yiming Du,Haobo Li,Xiyu Ren,Ginny Wong,Simon See,Lishu Luo,Haodong Duan,Pasquale Minervini,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出多模态检索头检测方法，发现仅4.4-10.2%的注意力头承担50%的检索质量；遮蔽前5%头导致MMLongBench-Doc和SlideVQA性能大幅下降；这些头可直接用于视觉文档排序，在MMDocIR上显著提升检索效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大视觉语言模型需要长上下文推理，但现有检索头检测方法仅适用于文本（基于复制标准），无法处理图像中的证据，因此需要一种多模态的检索头检测方法。

Method: 提出多模态检索头检测方法，根据问题token对文本或视觉证据的注意力分数来识别检索头；通过遮蔽实验验证其因果重要性；分析模态间共享性与动态特性；将检索头直接用于视觉文档排序。

Result: 多模态检索头稀疏（4.4-10.2%）、内在且因果重要；遮蔽前5%头部后MMLongBench-Doc从48.2%降至5.7%，SlideVQA从71.2%降至8.9%；在MMDocIR上Qwen3-VL-8B的Recall@1提升7.7/7.4（macro/micro）页面检索和6.3/6.8布局检索。

Conclusion: 多模态检索头是稀疏且关键的结构，图像检索头比文本检索头更动态；无需额外训练即可用于视觉文档排序，显著优于基线方法。

Abstract: Large vision-language models increasingly rely on long-context modeling to reason over documents, hour-level videos, and long-horizon agent trajectories, requiring them to locate relevant evidence across interleaved text and images. Prior work has studied this behavior using retrieval heads in large language models, but its copy-based criterion does not directly apply when evidence appears in images. We introduce a multimodal retrieval head detection method that scores attention from question tokens to textual or visual evidence. With this method, we show that multimodal retrieval heads are sparse, intrinsic, and causally important: only 4.4-10.2% of attention heads account for 50% of the positive retrieval-score mass, and masking the top-5% selected heads drops MMLongBench-Doc from 48.2% to 5.7% and SlideVQA from 71.2% to 8.9%, while random-head masking is far less damaging. Further analysis shows that these heads are partly shared across modalities yet remain dynamic within each modality, with image retrieval heads changing more than text retrieval heads as context length and haystack modality change. Without further training, we find that these heads can also be used directly to rank visually rich documents: on MMDocIR, Qwen3-VL-8B selected-head scoring improves Recall@1 by 7.7/7.4 macro/micro points for page retrieval and 6.3/6.8 points for layout retrieval over the strongest reported baseline.

</details>


### [114] [A Dynamic Programming Framework for Discovering Count and Values of Multilevel Image Thresholding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27287)
*Eslam Hegazy,Mohamed Gabr*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于动态规划（DP）和最小误差阈值（MET）改进准则的多级图像阈值分割方法（MET-DP），能够自动确定合适的阈值数量，处理速度快于传统DP方法，但在SSIM和PSNR指标上不如人工设定阈值数量的传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数阈值分割方法需要用户手动输入阈值数量，缺乏自适应能力。因此，亟需一种能够根据图像内容自动确定合适阈值数量的方法，以提高阈值分割的自动化程度和适用性。

Method: 提出MET-DP方法：结合动态规划算法与改进的最小误差阈值（MET）准则，通过经验统计研究验证方法优越性，并在自然图像、卫星图像和医学图像等综合数据集上与现有方法进行对比。

Result: 在高阈值数量下，MET-DP方法的运行时间远低于传统动态规划阈值方法；对于大多数不同类型的测试图像，该方法能自动检测出合适的阈值数量；但在SSIM和PSNR指标上，传统人工设定阈值数量的方法优于MET-DP。

Conclusion: MET-DP在自动确定阈值数量和计算效率方面具有优势，适合需要自动化的场景；但在图像重建质量（SSIM/PSNR）上仍有提升空间，未来可进一步优化准则以接近人工设定阈值方法的性能。

Abstract: Multilevel Image thresholding is an important preprocessing algorithm in computer vision applications nowadays. Since most common thresholding methods take the desired count of thresholds as input by the user, thresholding methods that automatically determines a suitable count of thresholds from the input image itself are advantageous. In this article, a novel thresholding method based on a dynamic programming algorithm and a modification of Minimum Error Thresholding (MET) criterion is thoroughly presented. An empirical statistical study is performed to pinpoint why this proposed method is superior. Moreover, an extended comparison between this proposed method and other state-of-the-art methods is performed on a comprehensive set of natural, satellite and medical test images. The numerical results show that the proposed MET-DP method takes much less time than traditional dynamic programming thresholding methods when the number of thresholds is high. The proposed method can detect a suitable count of thresholds for most of tested images of different types. However, traditional methods that take the count of thresholds as input produce thresholded images of higher structural similarity index measure (SSIM) and peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) values than MET-DP. Source code can be found on https://w3id.org/met-dp/article1-code

</details>


### [115] [Gemini Embedding 2: A Native Multimodal Embedding Model from Gemini](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27295)
*Madhuri Shanbhogue,Zhe Li,Shanfeng Zhang,Gustavo Hernández Ábrego,Shih-Cheng Huang,Aashi Jain,Daniel Salz,Sonam Goenka,Chaitra Hegde,Ji Ma,Feiyang Chen,Jiaxing Wu,Tanmaya Dabral,Babak Samari,Kevin Poulet,Daniel Cer,Kaifeng Chen,Paul Suganathan,Hui Hui,Jovan Andonov,Philippe Schlattner,Jay Han,Iftekhar Naim,Wing Lowe,Vladimir Pchelin,Albert Yang,Yi-Ting Chen,Zhongli Ding,Grace Zhang,Georg Heigold,Yichang Chen,Antoine Reveillon,Brendan Mccloskey,Wenlei Zhou,Dahun Kim,Rui Meng,Emma Wang,Jack Zheng,Halley Fede,Zhen Yang,Keegan Mosley,Brian Potetz,Sahil Dua,Henrique Schechter Vera,Shen Gao,Hesen Zhang,Andreas Hess,Hengxuan Ying,Alberto Montes,Karan Gill,Min Choi,Sebastian Russo,Anja Hauth,Jinhyuk Lee,Michael Boratko,Megan Barnes,Vikram Rao,Claudiu Musat,Cyril Allauzen,Ehsan Variani,Shankar Kumar,Tom Bagby,Junyi Jiao,Yang Gu,Tengxin Li,Ayush Agrawal,Roberto Santana,Dev Nath,Stephen Karukas,Shuoxuan Han,Lucia Loher,Alice Twu,Nidhi Vyas,Siddharth Bhai,Frank Palma Gomez,Wangyuan Zhang,Chaoren Liu,Jizheng Yang,Steve Qiu,Shijie Zhang,Sujay Kulkarni,Sascha Rothe,Sean Nakamoto,Raphael Hoffmann,Zach Gleicher,Yunhsuan Sung,Qin Yin,Tom Duerig,Mojtaba Seyedhosseini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Gemini Embedding 2 是一个原生多模态嵌入模型，能将视频、音频、图像和文本统一映射到同一表示空间，通过大规模对比学习在多任务多阶段训练下，在多项检索基准上达到SOTA，零样本泛化能力优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有嵌入模型通常针对单一模态设计，缺乏统一的多模态表示能力，难以处理视频、音频、图像和文本交错输入的复杂场景，因此需要构建一个原生多模态嵌入模型。

Method: 利用Gemini的多模态能力，采用大规模对比学习，设计多任务多阶段训练流程，支持任意组合的交错输入，实现视频、音频、图像和文本的统一嵌入表示。

Result: 在MSCOCO上R@1达62.9，Vatex上NDCG@10达68.8，MTEB多语言达69.9，MTEB代码达84.0，超越专门模型；在从天文学到烹饪等跨领域任务中表现优异。

Conclusion: Gemini Embedding 2 作为统一多模态嵌入模型，在多种检索任务上全面超越专用模型，零样本能力强，可广泛应用于RAG、推荐、搜索等下游任务，是通用可靠的表示方案。

Abstract: We introduce Gemini Embedding 2, a native multimodal embedding model that allows embedding video, audio, image, and text modalities in a unified representation space. We leverage the multimodal capabilities of Gemini to produce embeddings for arbitrary combinations of interleaved inputs across all these modalities that generalize well across a wide variety of tasks. Applying large-scale contrastive learning in a multi-task multi-stage training setup, we achieve state-of-the-art performance on key embedding benchmarks including unimodal, cross-modal, and multimodal retrieval spanning a diverse set of tasks. We show that our embedding model demonstrates strong performance (with a score of 62.9 R@1 on MSCOCO, 68.8 NDCG@10 on Vatex, 69.9 on MTEB multilingual and 84.0 on MTEB Code) across a variety of tasks surpassing the performance of specialized models. These unified capabilities make Gemini Embedding 2 a promising candidate for downstream use cases such as RAG, recommendation and search. Furthermore, its robust zero-shot performance across distinct fields - from astronomy and bioscience to fine arts and the culinary arts - establishes it as a highly reliable, out-of-the-box representation even for specialized domains.

</details>


### [116] [PlayClass: Automated Play Behaviour Classification in Poultry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27304)
*Prince Ravi Leow,Neil Scheidwasser,Rebecca Oscarsson,Per Jensen,Samir Bhatt,David Alejandro Duchêne*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PlayClass管道，利用SAM 3和YOLO进行长时间追踪，结合图像/视频基础模型的冻结嵌入，用于家禽玩耍行为分类。V-JEPA 2.1结合手工特征达到77.0%宏平均F1，但数据集仍因玩耍子类型与非玩耍行为运动特征相似及遮挡问题而具有挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动物福利自动化监测主要关注负面指标，对玩耍等积极福利行为研究不足，因此需要填补这一空白。

Method: 提出PlayClass管道：使用SAM 3结合YOLO引导的块边界进行长时间追踪以减少身份错误，利用图像和视频基础模型的冻结嵌入进行玩耍动作分类。

Result: 手工运动特征单独使用已达到竞争性准确率；V-JEPA 2.1在所有规模模型中表现最佳，结合手工特征达到77.0%宏平均F1。

Conclusion: 该工作为家禽玩耍行为分类的自动化框架提供了积极证据，但数据集仍因玩耍子类型与非玩耍行为运动特征相似及遮挡问题而具有挑战性。

Abstract: Automated monitoring of animal welfare has largely targeted negative indicators, leaving positive welfare behaviours such as play underexplored. To address this gap, we present PlayClass, a pipeline for play-behaviour classification in poultry from top-down pen video. The pipeline leverages long-duration tracking with SAM 3 via YOLO-guided chunk boundaries to minimise identity errors in point-based prompting, and frozen embeddings from image and video foundation models for play action classification. Although handcrafted motion features from tracked masks alone achieved competitive accuracy, V-JEPA 2.1 consistently outperformed all other backbones across model scales, reaching 77.0 macro-averaged F$_1$ when combined with handcrafted features. Despite this result, the dataset remains challenging due to play sub-types sharing similar kinematic profiles with non-play and inter-bird occlusion. Overall, our work provides encouraging evidence towards automated frameworks for play behaviour classification in poultry.

</details>


### [117] [How and What to Imagine? Visual Thinking in Unified Multimodal Models for Cross-View Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27310)
*Qian Yang,Ankur Sikarwar,Huy Le,Le Zhang,Zhuan Shi,Perouz Taslakian,Aishwarya Agrawal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出View Dropout（VDrop）训练策略，强制统一多模态模型（UMMs）在跨视角空间推理中利用中间思维图像而非仅依赖语言；比较了三种视觉思维形式（俯视、全景、点匹配），发现全景视觉思维结合VDrop在五个真实世界跨领域基准上取得最佳泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在跨视角空间推理中依赖语言而忽略细粒度几何信息，即使生成中间思维图像，模型也常忽视其中的视觉证据。因此需要让视觉思维真正发挥作用，并探索哪种视觉思维形式最有效。

Method: 在统一多模态模型中，提出View Dropout（VDrop）训练策略：在训练时隐藏一个输入视图的部分信息（对其答案span不可见，但对思维图像token可见），强制模型利用思维图像回答；在此基础上，从可学习性与信息量的权衡出发，比较三种思维图像变体（俯视图、全景图、点匹配图）。

Result: 在合成场景训练、五个真实世界跨领域基准测试上，全景视觉思维+VDrop是唯一同时具备信息量和可学习性的配置，取得最佳跨域泛化性能。

Conclusion: 通过VDrop强制模型使用思维图像，并选择全景图作为视觉思维形式，能有效提升VLM在跨视角空间推理任务上的表现和泛化能力。

Abstract: Cross-view spatial reasoning remains a weak spot for vision-language models (VLMs): they often reason in language and lose the fine-grained geometry needed for the task. Thinking with images aims to address this by generating an intermediate thinking image, but recent work shows that models often ignore the visual evidence in these traces. We therefore ask how to make visual thinking matter, and what kind of visual thinking works best. We study these questions in unified multimodal models (UMMs), which natively support interleaved image-text generation. For the first question, we propose View Dropout (VDrop), a training-time intervention that hides parts of one input view from the answer span while keeping them visible to the thinking-image tokens. This encourages the model to use the thinking image when answering, instead of relying only on the input views. Once the thinking image is used for answer prediction, we study which type of visual thinking is most effective. We frame this as a learnability-informativeness tradeoff and compare three thinking-image variants: top-down, panoramic, and point-matching renderings. Trained on synthetic scenes and evaluated on five real-world out-of-domain benchmarks, panoramic visual thinking with VDrop is the only configuration that is both informative and learnable, and it achieves the best out-of-domain generalization.

</details>


### [118] [Q-GeoMem: Question-Guided Geometric Memory for Video Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27318)
*Xianqiang Gao,Qizhi Chen,Delin Qu,Haoming Song,Zhigang Wang,Bin Zhao,Dong Wang,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种问题引导的几何记忆框架，通过双记忆库（细粒度上下文库和语义-几何证据库）和基于Q-Former的评分机制，提升了视频空间推理的性能，在VSI-Bench和VSTI-Bench上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频空间语言模型将记忆视为通用时间缓存，会引入冗余或无关的几何信息，削弱长程推理能力。

Method: 提出问题引导的几何记忆框架，包含：(1) 将相机条件几何注入视觉token；(2) 双记忆结构：细粒度上下文库（近期密集特征）和语义-几何证据库（紧凑长程证据）；(3) Q-Former评分机制：基于问题相关性和新颖性对候选帧评分；(4) 容量替换规则和自适应融合策略。

Result: 在VSI-Bench和VSTI-Bench基准上达到空间推理模型中的最优性能，消融实验验证了证据评分机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 问题引导的几何记忆框架能够有效提升视频空间推理性能，验证了选择性记忆和几何信息注入的重要性。

Abstract: Video spatial reasoning requires accumulating viewpoint-dependent evidence over time while retaining information useful to the question being asked. Existing spatial video-language models improve geometric perception and long-range context modeling, but often treat memory as a generic temporal cache, which can introduce redundant or irrelevant geometry and weaken long-horizon reasoning. We propose \textbf{\ours}, a question-guided geometric memory framework for video spatial reasoning. \ours injects camera-conditioned geometry into visual tokens and maintains two complementary memories: a Fine-Grained Context Bank for recent dense features and camera states, and a Semantic-Geometric Evidence Bank for compact long-range evidence. Each candidate frame is scored by the product of Q-Former-based question relevance and novelty with respect to the retained bank; this score is stored and reused during reading, while a capacity-based replacement rule keeps the bank compact. During reasoning, both memories are read before update and adaptively fused with the current frame representation. Experiments on VSI-Bench and VSTI-Bench show that \ours achieves state-of-the-art performance among evaluated spatial reasoning models, validating the effectiveness of question-guided geometric memory. Ablations further verify the contribution of the proposed evidence scoring mechanism.

</details>


### [119] [PARE: Pruning and Adaptive Routing for Efficient Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27336)
*Yutong Wang,Yunke Wang,Tianfan Xue,Yu Qiao,Yaohui Wang,Xinyuan Chen,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PARE是一种联合压缩宽度和深度的高效视频生成方法，通过结构感知剪枝（区分时空注意力头）和输入自适应路由（动态选择每步执行块），在Wan2.1-14B上显著减少计算量且保持质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频DiT模型计算开销大，现有方法采用固定的压缩架构，无法适应不同输入或去噪阶段，缺乏灵活性。

Method: 1) 宽度压缩：设计区分时空注意力头的重要性评分，避免过早剪枝运动关键的时间头；2) 深度压缩：训练轻量路由器，根据去噪时间步和视觉内容动态选择每步执行的块；3) 渐进式流程：先通过蒸馏恢复宽度剪枝质量，再联合优化学生网络和路由器。

Result: 在Wan2.1-14B上，PARE显著降低每步计算量，同时保持VBench各维度的生成质量，并可组合步蒸馏进一步加速。

Conclusion: PARE通过输入自适应的动态路由和结构感知剪枝，实现了比固定压缩架构更灵活高效的视频生成加速。

Abstract: Video Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) generate high-quality videos but demand substantial compute due to wide blocks, deep architectures, and iterative sampling. Recent methods reduce cost by compressing width, depth, or sampling steps, but typically commit to a fixed architecture that cannot adapt to individual inputs or denoising stages. We propose PARE (Pruning and Adaptive Routing for Efficient video generation), which jointly compresses width and depth with structure-aware pruning and input-adaptive routing. For width, we observe that attention heads specialize into spatial and temporal roles, and design importance scoring that accounts for this distinction to prevent motion-critical temporal heads from being pruned prematurely. For depth, we train a lightweight router conditioned on denoising timestep and visual content to dynamically select which blocks to execute at each step, enabling per-input compute adaptation rather than static block removal. A progressive pipeline first recovers width-pruned quality via distillation, then jointly optimizes the student and router to decouple the two learning objectives. Experiments on Wan2.1-14B for both image-to-video and text-to-video generation show that PARE substantially reduces per-step computation while preserving quality across VBench dimensions, and composes with step distillation for further acceleration.

</details>


### [120] [Towards Controllable Image Generation through Representation-Conditioned Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27343)
*Nithesh Chandher Karthikeyan,Jonas Unger,Gabriel Eilertsen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探索使用预训练自监督模型的表示来条件化扩散模型，替代传统文本或语义图条件，无需标注数据，能提升无条件生成质量并提供可控的表示空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统扩散模型依赖文本提示或语义图等条件机制，需要大量标注数据，成本高且灵活度有限。本文旨在探索无需标注的自监督条件化方法。

Method: 使用预训练自监督模型的表示作为扩散模型的条件信号，替代传统条件机制；并在该表示空间中识别变化方向以实现生成控制。

Result: 自条件机制提升了无条件图像生成的质量；表示空间展现出良好的平滑性和解耦性，具备可控生成潜力。

Conclusion: 自监督条件化为扩散模型提供了一种无需标注数据的可控生成新思路，表示空间具有良好的属性，值得进一步探索。

Abstract: Diffusion models have emerged as powerful tools for high-quality image generation and editing, but guiding these models to produce specific outputs remains a challenge. Conventional approaches rely on conditioning mechanisms, such as text prompts or semantic maps, which require extensively annotated datasets. In this preliminary work, we explore diffusion models conditioned on representations from a pre-trained self-supervised model. The self-conditioning mechanism not only improves the quality of unconditional image generation, but also provides a representation space that can be used to control the generation. We explore this conditioning space by identifying directions of variations, and demonstrate promising properties in terms of smoothness and disentanglement.

</details>


### [121] [Feedforward 3D Editing Learns from Semantic-Part Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27351)
*Jiawei Weng,Saining Zhang,Zhenxin Diao,Peishuo Li,Henghaofan Zhang,Junhao Chen,Hao Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于语义部件变换的大规模前馈3D编辑方法，构建了超10万对编辑数据集的Pxform，并设计了无需推理时3D遮罩的PartFlow网络，在几何与外观编辑任务上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈式3D编辑面临缺乏高质量成对监督数据的核心挑战，现有数据集依赖独立生成资产或图像重建，导致编辑定位不准、保真度差、边界模糊及语义一致性有限。

Method: 1) 构建Pxform数据集：基于语义部件变换，生成超10万对高质量前后编辑对，涵盖7种编辑类型。2) 提出PartFlow网络：将源感知隐空间控制注入预训练3D生成先验，引入遮罩感知速度保持与渲染空间一致性监督，推理时无需3D编辑遮罩。

Result: 在几何编辑与外观编辑基准上均达到SOTA性能，验证了语义部件级监督对可扩展3D编辑的有效性。

Conclusion: 高质量的语义部件监督能够显著提升前馈式3D编辑的可扩展性，PartFlow在编辑保真度与源物体保持之间实现了良好平衡。

Abstract: 3D editing is a fundamental capability for scalable 3D content creation. While image editing has rapidly evolved toward large-scale feedforward generative paradigms, 3D AI generation remains dominated by training-free editing pipelines. A central challenge of feedforward 3D editing lies in the lack of high-quality paired supervision. Editable 3D assets require simultaneous preservation of geometry, multi-view consistency, structural coherence, and localized edit controllability. Existing 3D editing datasets often rely on independently generated assets, image-mediated reconstruction or narrow edit taxonomies, leading to inaccurate localization, weak preservation, blurred edit boundaries, and limited semantic consistency. In this work, we introduce a new perspective: scalable feedforward 3D editing should be learned from semantic-part transformations. Based on this insight, we propose Pxform, a high-quality 3D editing dataset with over 100K consistent before/after editing pairs across seven edit types. Instead of treating objects as unstructured shapes, our pipeline grounds edits directly in semantic 3D parts. Built upon Pxform, we further propose PartFlow, a feedforward 3D editing network that injects source-aware latent control into pretrained 3D generative priors. PartFlow introduces mask-aware velocity preservation and render-space consistency supervision to jointly improve edit fidelity and source preservation, while requiring no 3D edit mask during inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate that high-quality semantic-part supervision substantially improves scalable 3D editing, enabling PartFlow to achieve state-of-the-art performance on both geometric and appearance editing benchmarks.

</details>


### [122] [SpatialBench: Is Your Spatial Foundation Model an All-Round Player?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27367)
*Haosong Peng,Hao Li,Jiaqi Chen,Yuhao Pan,Runmao Yao,Yalun Dai,Fushuo Huo,Fangzhou Hong,Zhaoxi Chen,Haozhao Wang,Dingwen Zhang,Ziwei Liu,Wenchao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SpatialBench——一个跨范式、多领域的空间基础模型综合基准，包含19个数据集、546个场景、41个模型、6种范式、5个任务套件和4种输入密度设置。全面评估后发现当前模型尚未达到全能水平，并揭示了关键洞见：全局注意力机制最大化精度，有界记忆策略实现长序列可扩展性，领域对齐和数据质量比简单数据规模扩展更重要。同时引入大规模数据集DA-Next-5M和强基线模型DA-Next。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间基础模型评估局限于特定领域和特定设计的任务，缺乏对模型跨不同下游任务、视角、场景域、输入密度和硬件约束的鲁棒泛化能力的全面评估。

Method: 构建SpatialBench基准，采用确定性采样策略，覆盖19个数据集、546个场景、5个空间领域，系统评估41个模型在6种范式、5个任务套件和4种输入密度设置上的表现。同时收集最大数据缺口，构建DA-Next-5M大规模数据集和DA-Next基线模型。

Result: 当前空间基础模型尚不是全能型选手；全局注意力机制最大化精度，有界记忆策略解锁长序列可扩展性；领域对齐和数据质量比简单的数据规模扩展更为关键。

Conclusion: 空间基础模型仍需进一步发展以实现真正的鲁棒泛化；未来研究应注重领域对齐和高数据质量，而非单纯扩大数据规模；所提出的SpatialBench、DA-Next-5M和DA-Next为后续研究提供了重要资源。

Abstract: While spatial foundation models have demonstrated impressive performance on standard datasets, a critical question remains: are they truly all-round players capable of generalizing robustly across diverse downstream tasks, arbitrary viewpoints, shifting scene domains, varying input densities, and specific hardware constraints? Answering this overarching question requires a holistic assessment, yet current models are mainly evaluated on specific domains for which they were specifically designed or trained. Such evaluations are intrinsically limited by narrow paradigm coverage, limited scene domains, and arbitrary frame sampling, making it fundamentally difficult to assess their true generalization capabilities. To address this gap, we present SpatialBench, a cross-paradigm, domain-diverse benchmark for spatial foundation models with deterministic sampling. SpatialBench features unprecedented scale and rigorous deterministic design, comprising 19 datasets and 546 scenes across 5 diverse spatial domains. It comprehensively evaluates 41 models across 6 paradigms on 5 task suites under 4 different input density settings. Our extensive evaluation reveals that current models are not yet all-round players, and uncovers crucial insights for future advancement. Specifically, we demonstrate that full-context attention maximizes accuracy while bounded-memory strategies unlock long-sequence scalability. Moreover, our empirical evaluations in challenging embodied and egocentric tasks demonstrate that strict domain alignment and high data quality are far more critical to performance than simple dataset scaling. Furthermore, to address the largest data gap identified in our analysis, we go beyond evaluation by introducing a large-scale dataset, DA-Next-5M, and a strong baseline model, DA-Next, pushing the boundaries of spatial representation learning.

</details>


### [123] [G3T Up! Gravity Aligned Coordinate Frames Simplify Pointmap Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27372)
*Bharath Raj Nagoor Kani,Noah Snavely*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出G3T模型，通过预测重力对齐坐标系下的点云图，替代传统相机中心坐标系，提升3D重建精度，并引入G3T-Long增量重建流程进一步优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统前馈3D重建方法使用相机中心坐标系，该坐标系在不同视角间缺乏一致性，无法充分利用真实场景中普遍存在的结构先验（如重力方向），导致重建效果受限。

Method: 从现有模型微调，提出G3T（重力接地几何变换器），预测重力对齐坐标系下的直立点云图和相机到重力的位姿；并在此基础上构建G3T-Long子图增量重建流程。

Result: G3T产生高精度的重力感知预测；G3T-Long利用降低的旋转自由度显著提升重建精度。

Conclusion: 重力对齐坐标系相比相机中心坐标系在3D重建任务中具有优势，能利用场景结构先验，减少视角间的旋转自由度，提升重建准确度。

Abstract: Modern feed-forward 3D reconstruction methods like VGGT predict pixel-aligned pointmaps in camera-centric coordinate frames. However, this choice of coordinate frame is not always optimal. We propose instead to predict pointmaps in upright, gravity-aligned frames that exploit strong structural cues present in many real-world scenes. Unlike camera-centric frames, gravity-aligned frames share a common vertical axis across viewpoints, reducing the rotational degrees of freedom needed to relate pointmaps to one another. To this end, we introduce the Gravity Grounded Geometry Transformer (G3T), fine-tuned from existing models on gravity-aligned 3D data. G3T produces highly accurate gravity-aware predictions, including upright pointmaps and camera-to-gravity poses. We further introduce G3T-Long, a submap-based incremental 3D reconstruction pipeline that leverages the reduced rotational degrees of freedom afforded by upright frames to achieve significantly improved reconstruction accuracy.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [When Does Adaptive Guidance Help? Belief-Aware Privileged Distillation for Autonomous Driving Under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26155)
*Mehmet Haklidir*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BA-GSAC提出用集成不确定性动态调节知识蒸馏系数，但在严重部分可观测场景下，集成对观测盲区不敏感，导致自适应系数失效；简单线性衰减策略效果反而更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GSAC使用固定蒸馏系数，忽略了智能体在不同状态下的不确定性差异。

Method: 通过集成模型的预测差异（ensemble disagreement）来动态调整蒸馏系数λ，并在Highway-Env的不同POMDP难度级别上进行系统性比较。

Result: 在轻度/中度部分可观测下自适应策略有优势；但在严重遮挡下，自适应系数迅速坍缩至最小值，而简单的确定性线性衰减策略在所有指标上最优。

Conclusion: 调度效应（而非集成感知）是稳定性提升的关键；集成预测目标的选择（预测部分观测 vs. 全状态）是重要的设计考量。

Abstract: Guided Soft Actor-Critic (GSAC) distills knowledge from a privileged full-state teacher to a partial-observation student for autonomous driving, but uses a fixed distillation coefficient lambda regardless of the agent's uncertainty. We present Belief-Aware GSAC (BA-GSAC), which modulates lambda via ensemble disagreement, and use it as a testbed for a systematic empirical study asking: when does adaptive guidance actually help? Evaluating five strategies (fixed lambda in {0.01, 0.1}, adaptive, linear decay, and vanilla SAC) across three POMDP difficulty levels on Highway-Env, we find that preliminary single-seed runs suggest benefits under mild and moderate partial observability, but under severe occlusion (evaluated with 3 seeds for all methods) the adaptive coefficient collapses to lambda_min within about 3K steps. We trace this to an observability blindness phenomenon: because the ensemble predicts partial observations, it achieves low disagreement even under heavy occlusion, modeling what is visible but unable to detect what is missing. We diagnose the root cause and propose an architectural fix (training the ensemble on full-state predictions using the guiding actor's privileged access); while not validated here, we show that even with current limitations, the warmup phase provides measurable stabilization (CV=13.3% vs. 29.8% for constant lambda=0.01). In fact, a simple deterministic linear decay schedule achieves the best severe-POMDP performance across all metrics (mean 116.5, CV=8.9%), suggesting that the scheduling effect, not the ensemble, drives the stability benefit. These findings provide practical guidance for designing uncertainty-aware teacher-student frameworks and highlight ensemble prediction targets as an important design choice.

</details>


### [125] [PhyPush: One Push is All You Need for Sensorless Physical Property Estimation with Physics-Guided Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26284)
*Koyo Fujii,Luis Figueredo,Praminda Caleb-Solly,Ivan Boschi,Edoardo Ida',Marco Carricato,Aly Magassouba*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhyPush是一个基于物理引导的Transformer框架，仅利用单次推动中从标准机械臂获取的运动学数据（末端执行器速度），就能准确估计物体的质量和摩擦系数，在仿真和真实实验中均优于依赖力传感器等专用硬件的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖力/扭矩传感器、触觉阵列或多相机运动捕捉系统等专用硬件，成本高、可扩展性差，限制了实际部署。因此，作者希望仅利用标准机械臂上容易获取的运动学数据来实现物理属性估计。

Method: 提出PhyPush框架，核心是一个物理引导的Transformer模型。输入为单次推动中末端执行器的速度（运动学数据），模型通过物理引导损失函数将牛顿第二定律和库仑摩擦模型作为约束融入学习过程，从而增强物理一致性和泛化能力。

Result: 在仿真中，PhyPush相比有权访问完整力信息的基线方法，误差降低了超过10%。在真实实验中，它优于使用数据驱动损失的方法。在具有挑战性的领域外条件下，质量和摩擦估计均更准确。

Conclusion: 物理引导的机器学习方法可以在无需专用传感器的情况下，仅依靠单次推动和运动学数据实现低成本、传感器高效的物理属性估计，验证了物理先验在引导数据高效学习中的价值。

Abstract: Accurately estimating object mass and friction is fundamental to achieving reliable and adaptive robotic manipulation. Although interactive perception provides a powerful mechanism for inferring such properties, most existing approaches depend on specialized hardware such as force/torque sensors, tactile arrays, or multi-camera motion-capture systems, limiting scalability and deployment. This paper presents PhyPush, a physics-guided Transformer framework that estimates an object's mass and friction coefficient using only kinematically derived end-effector velocity from a single push. This typically requires data available on standard robotic arms. The model incorporates constraints from Newton's second law and the Coulomb friction model through a physics-guided loss, improving physical consistency and generalization to unseen objects and surfaces. Across diverse simulation and real-world setups, PhyPush consistently achieves more accurate mass and friction estimation in challenging out-of-domain conditions. In simulation, it reduces error by over 10% compared with a baseline that has privileged access to full force information, while in real-world experiments, it outperforms a data-driven loss approach. Overall, the results demonstrate that physics-guided learning can enable low-cost, sensor-efficient estimation of physical properties, relying solely on a single push and readily available kinematic data.

</details>


### [126] [Collaborative Navigation and Exploration with $β$-Sparse Gaussian Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26304)
*Evangelos Psomiadis,Dipankar Maity,Panagiotis Tsiotras*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种异构机器人在未知环境中的协作导航框架，通过β-稀疏高斯过程实现任务感知的诱导点选择，在带宽限制下联合优化地图传输与导航动作，显著降低路径成本和通信量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决异构机器人在未知环境中协作导航时面临的感知、通信和计算资源限制问题，尤其是带宽约束下如何高效传输环境信息以辅助导航。

Method: 提出了β-稀疏高斯过程（β-Sparse Gaussian Processes）用于任务感知的诱导点选择；设计了平衡任务相关性与探索的动作选择策略；实现了地图点选择与导航动作的在线联合优化。

Result: 在火星和地球地图仿真中，框架相比无通信基线降低了18%的路径成本，相比原始数据传输基线减少了76%的传输信息量。

Conclusion: 通过稀疏化信息传输和任务感知优化，该框架在资源受限条件下显著提升了异构机器人协作导航的效率，验证了通信高效性与导航性能之间的有效权衡。

Abstract: Collaborative navigation of heterogeneous robots in unknown environments poses significant challenges due to sensing, communication, and computational limitations. In this work, a lead robot navigates toward a target while a mobile sensor robot (e.g., a drone) assists by transmitting information about its locally observed environment under bandwidth constraints. We propose a framework that enables the sensor to jointly select its transmitted map points and navigation actions online, while also predicting unexplored regions of the environment. To this end, we present $β$-Sparse Gaussian Processes, a novel and robust variational sparse Gaussian Process model for task-aware inducing point selection. Furthermore, we develop an action-selection strategy that balances task relevance with exploration. Simulations on Mars and Earth maps show that the framework can reduce path cost by 18% relative to no communication and decrease transmitted information by 76% compared to raw-data transmission baselines.

</details>


### [127] [NightSight: Passive Computation for Navigation in Dark Using Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26330)
*Deepak Singh,Brijan Vaghasiya,Shreyas Khobragade,Nitin Sanket*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级感知方法，结合单目事件相机、编码孔径镜头和红外点阵投影器，使小型空中机器人在完全黑暗环境中实现自主导航和深度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型空中机器人难以搭载笨重的感知系统，在完全黑暗环境中自主导航仍是重大挑战。

Method: 通过编码孔径对红外点阵投影产生深度相关的模糊特征，训练CNN从这些特征解码密集深度图，仅用合成数据训练。

Result: 零样本泛化到真实场景，实时20Hz运行在Jetson Orin Nano上，2.5m范围内L1误差7.0cm（2.80%误差）。

Conclusion: 结合结构光照明、编码光学和事件相机，可在完全黑暗中实现鲁棒的感知与导航，适合资源受限平台。

Abstract: Small aerial robots are particularly well-suited for search and rescue in confined and hazardous environments due to their agility, low cost, and ability to traverse through cluttered spaces that are inaccessible to larger platforms. However, enabling autonomous navigation in complete darkness remains a significant challenge, because small aerial robots cannot easily accommodate perception systems that demand substantial payload, power, or computation. In this work, we present a lightweight perception approach that combines a monocular event camera, a coded aperture lens, and an infrared dot projector to enable navigation in such conditions. The projected pattern, when imaged through the coded aperture, produces depth dependent blur signatures that implicitly encode scene geometry. We train a convolutional neural network to decode these signatures into dense depth maps using only synthetic data generated from a simple planar wall setup. Despite this minimal training regime, the model generalizes zero-shot to complex real-world scenes. Our system operates in real time at 20 Hz on a NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano, demonstrating suitability for resource-constrained platforms. We further analyze the impact of different coded aperture designs on depth estimation performance. Our approach gives high accuracy (l1 error 7.0cm) upto 2.5m range (2.80% error). These results highlight the potential of combining structured illumination, coded optics, and event-based sensing for enabling robust perception and navigation in complete darkness.

</details>


### [128] [RCSP: Risk-Sensitive Conjectural Scenario Planning for Safe Dynamic Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26348)
*Zhengye Han,Quanyan Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出风险敏感推测性场景规划（RCSP），一种用于移动机器人在动态障碍物环境中避免“预测性近失碰撞”问题的规划层。RCSP通过维护轻量级运动推测信念、采样未来交互、惩罚高风险尾部并通过局部安全检查执行。在MuJoCo、ROS2/Gazebo和DynaBARN/Jackal上的实验表明，RCSP相比非自适应预测器提高了次级安全性和路径质量，但在严格基准成功率上不及调参后的DWA和TEB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人在碰撞前就可能失败：当前安全的运动速度可能将机器人束缚在即将被移动障碍物封闭的通道中。这种“预测性近失承诺”问题需要前瞻性风险评估能力。

Method: RCSP包含三个核心环节：1) 维护对局部运动推测的轻量级信念分布；2) 采样短时域的未来交互场景；3) 对高风险尾部分布进行惩罚，并通过局部安全检查执行所选命令。

Result: 在MuJoCo瓶颈任务中，RCSP无碰撞到达目标，次级安全性和路径质量点估计优于非自适应预测器（但延迟增加）；在ROS2/Gazebo中，将RCSP的局部安全层添加到Nav2栈可减少动态近失失败；在官方DynaBARN/Jackal迁移测试中，调参后的DWA和TEB在严格基准成功率上更强，揭示了RCSP的边界。

Conclusion: RCSP可作为预测风险模块补充现有导航栈，在动态瓶颈场景中提升安全性，但其在严格成功率上尚不及传统方法，适合作为安全增强层而非替代方案。

Abstract: Mobile robots can fail before they collide: a velocity that is safe now may commit the robot to a passage that moving obstacles will soon close. We study this predictive near-miss commitment problem and propose Risk-Sensitive Conjectural Scenario Planning (RCSP), a planning layer that evaluates candidate commands against plausible short-horizon obstacle futures. RCSP maintains a lightweight belief over local motion conjectures, samples future interactions, penalizes high-risk tails, and executes through a local safety check. In controlled MuJoCo bottleneck tasks, the RCSP planner reaches the goal without collisions and yields higher secondary safety and path-quality point estimates than a non-adaptive predictor, with additional latency. In ROS2/Gazebo, adding the local safety layer to a standard Nav2 stack reduces dynamic near-miss failures. On official DynaBARN/Jackal transfer, tuned DWA and TEB remain stronger on strict benchmark success, revealing the boundary of the approach. These simulation results position RCSP as a predictive-risk module that complements existing navigation stacks in dynamic bottleneck regimes.

</details>


### [129] [Closing the Loop in Teleoperation: Episode-Level Data Quality Assessment and Feedback for High-Quality Demonstration Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26349)
*Gokul Narayanan,Yash Shahapurkar,Melih Erdogan,Brian Zhu,Eugen Solowjow*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DQAF框架，在遥操作数据收集中提供即时后验反馈（而非简单成功/失败），帮助新手操作员更快提升演示质量，实验表明接受反馈的操作员改进速度更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业自动化正从刚性系统转向灵活系统，需要大规模真实机器人演示数据，但新手操作员收集的遥操作演示常存在次优行为（低效运动、重复修正、接近关节极限），现有方法仅提供二元成功/失败反馈，缺乏解释性指导。

Method: 提出DQAF框架，在遥操作后提取子任务进度、运动平滑度、停顿、运动学极限等质量信号，转化为结构化质量评估和可操作的自然语言反馈，形成闭环反馈机制。

Result: 验证研究中系统与人类评审员在拒绝原因和可操作反馈上一致；三项用户研究中，接受即时自动反馈的操作员比未接受者更快产生高质量演示。

Conclusion: DQAF框架通过提供语义级、解释性的后验反馈显著加速了新手操作员的学习曲线，对大规模高质量遥操作数据收集具有重要实用价值。

Abstract: Industrial automation is at a pivotal moment, as Physical AI is driving a transition from rigid, hand-engineered automation systems toward more flexible and adaptive systems. This shift has created a growing demand for large-scale, real-world robot demonstration data, making teleoperation an increasingly important mechanism for data collection. However, high-quality teleoperated demonstrations remain difficult to obtain in practice, as novice operators often produce episodes that are task-successful but suboptimal for downstream use due to inefficient motion, repeated corrections, or operation near robot joint limits. We present a Data Quality Assessment and Feedback (DQAF) framework that closes the loop in teleoperation by providing immediate post-episode feedback grounded in semantic task progress and robot telemetry. The framework extracts quality relevant signals such as sub-task progress, motion smoothness, stalls, kinematic limits and converts them into structured quality assessments and actionable natural-language feedback. Unlike binary success or failure feedback, the proposed system explains why an episode is suboptimal and highlights specific behaviors to correct in the next trial. We evaluate the framework through a diagnostic validation study and a pilot user study. In the validation study, the system is compared with a human reviewer during dataset curation, producing rejection reasons and actionable feedback for improvement. In the pilot study with three novice operators across two manipulation tasks, the operator who received the systems immediate, automated post-episode feedback improved faster than those who did not, producing higher-quality demonstrations sooner.

</details>


### [130] [Multi-Robot Box Transport over Different Surfaces with Decentralized Role-based Proportional Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26430)
*Aditya Bhatt,Himavarshini Yarragangu,Urvish Shah,Venkata Sai Yaswanth Mohan Thota,Souma Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了R2P2（Roles with Rules and Proportional-control Primitive）方法，这是一种异步去中心化的任务与运动规划方法，用于多机器人协作推箱任务。该方法通过为机器人分配不同角色（推、支撑、阻止），并基于规则或比例控制实现机器人在不同地形（平地、上坡、下坡）和摩擦条件下的协作运输。在六机器人仿真（NVIDIA IsaacSim）和四机器人实物实验（TurtleBot）中验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人协作推箱在建筑、仓库、灾后清理等场景中具有广泛应用价值，但在不同倾斜角度和摩擦系数的地面上实现协作运输面临独特挑战。需要一种去中心化的方法，以减轻通信、同步和共识需求，并避免单点故障问题。

Method: 提出R2P2方法：1）基于规则为机器人分配角色（推、支撑、阻止），角色取决于操作模式（箱子旋转vs平移）；2）根据角色采用基于规则的控制或比例控制机器人速度；3）每个机器人仅需观测自身和箱子的位置与朝向；4）完全异步去中心化架构。

Result: 在NVIDIA IsaacSim仿真中部署六机器人团队，验证了方法在不同表面摩擦/倾斜和箱子质量场景下的泛化能力，成功率优于标准的虚拟领导者-跟随者方法。并在四台TurtleBot上完成了搬运1.2kg箱子的实物实验验证。

Conclusion: R2P2作为一种去中心化的角色分配与比例控制相结合的方案，能有效应对不同地形和摩擦条件下的多机器人协作推箱任务，具有去中心化带来的鲁棒性优势，且仿真与实物实验均验证了其可行性和优越性。

Abstract: Collaborative transport of objects via pushing by multiple robots has many applications, ranging from construction and warehouse environments to post disaster debris clean-up. Achieving collaborative transport over surfaces with different inclination and friction properties however poses unique challenges. To address these challenges, this paper presents an asynchronous decentralized task and motion planning approach for transporting rectangular boxes of varying mass over flat, uphill and downhill terrain. Such a decentralized approach alleviates communication, synchronization and consensus needs and mitigates single point of failure issues. Our approach, called R2P2 or Roles with Rules and Proportional-control Primitive, assigns roles (e.g., push, support and prevent) to robots based on rules cognizant of the mode of manipulation needed (box rotation vs translation); this is followed by either rule-based control or proportional control of robot velocity based on the roles. Each robot is assumed to observe the location and heading of self and the box in executing the role and controls. R2P2 is evaluated with a six-robot team deployed in a simulator built using NVIDIA IsaacSim -- demonstrating generalizability across different surface friction/inclination and box mass scenarios, and better success rate compared to a standard virtual-leader-follower method. R2P2 is also successfully validated with a physical experiment, where it is executed onboard four turtlebots tasked with moving a 1.2 kg box.

</details>


### [131] [Robust Koopman Control Barrier Filters for Safe Actor-Critic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26452)
*Dhruv S. Kushwaha,Zoleikha A. Biron*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Robust Koopman-CBF SAC框架，通过数据驱动的Koopman预测器构建CBF安全约束，并用投影残差边界处理近似误差，在安全控制任务中实现零约束违反，同时揭示了速度势垒和线性EDMD模型的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于控制屏障函数（CBF）的安全强化学习方法依赖精确动力学模型和手工设计的屏障函数，无法直接应用于无模型RL场景。本文旨在弥合模型无关RL与可验证安全之间的鸿沟。

Method: 1) 从数据中学习有限维Koopman线性预测器；2) 在提升空间构建仿射CBF约束；3) 通过二次规划安全层强制执行约束；4) 利用留出轨迹数据估计投影残差边界，收紧CBF条件以对抗有限维近似误差；5) 评论家在执行的safe action上训练，演员正则化朝向Koopman-CBF可行集。

Result: 在CartPole稳定与跟踪任务中实现零约束违反，性能匹配或超越无约束SAC。在Safety Gymnasium高维任务中部分减少违反，但暴露出速度势垒与线性EDMD模型的局限性。

Conclusion: Robust Koopman-CBF过滤器是连接模型无关RL与可验证安全的有前途方向，但需要高阶与多步Koopman-CBF扩展来应对高维复杂场景。

Abstract: Safe reinforcement learning (RL) for robotic systems requires policies that improve task performance while satisfying state and input constraints during both training and deployment. Control barrier functions (CBFs) provide a principled mechanism for enforcing forward invariance through minimally invasive safety filters, but their use in model-free RL is limited by the need for accurate dynamics and hand-designed barrier certificates. We propose Robust Koopman-CBF SAC, a safety-filtered actor--critic framework that learns a finite-dimensional Koopman predictor from data, constructs affine CBF constraints in the lifted space, and enforces them through a quadratic-program safety layer. To account for finite-dimensional Koopman approximation error, the CBF condition is tightened using a projected residual margin estimated from held-out rollout data. The critic is trained on the executed safe action, while the actor is regularized toward the Koopman-CBF feasible set, reducing dependence on the filter over training. Across safe-control benchmarks, the method achieves zero constraint violations on CartPole stabilization and tracking while matching or exceeding unconstrained SAC returns. On high-dimensional Safety Gymnasium locomotion tasks, the method reduces violations in some settings but also exposes important limitations of first-order velocity barriers and linear EDMD models, motivating high-order and multi-step Koopman-CBF extensions. These results suggest that robust Koopman-CBF filters are a promising bridge between model-free RL and certifiable safety, while clarifying the structural conditions under which such filters remain effective. All code is available at \href{https://github.com/DhruvKushwaha/Koopman-CBF-Soft-Actor-Critic}{Github Repository}.

</details>


### [132] [Heterogeneous AAV Logistics Task Allocation: A Reinforcement Learning Enhanced Overlapping Coalition Formation Game Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26471)
*Yuze Zhou,Jingliang Sun,Junzhi Li,Jianxin Zhong,Zihan Wang,Teng Long*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对动态城市物流中异构AAV的随机时效任务分配问题，提出了一种强化学习增强的重叠联盟形成博弈方法，通过Transformer-based SAC网络学习自适应联盟更新策略，显著降低了广义物流成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态城市物流中，时间敏感任务的随机涌现对异构AAV物流任务分配的最优性构成重大挑战，现有启发式方法难以应对时变任务集。

Method: 建立动态任务分配模型，量化全局最优性为广义物流成本；设计Transformer-based Soft Actor-Critic网络，利用多头自注意力编码变长物流状态并捕获时空依赖；学习策略自适应指导重叠联盟形成博弈的联盟更新。

Result: 所提算法在32架AAV和80个任务场景下，相比启发式OCF基线实现39.76%的成本降低；联盟形成过程证明是精确势能博弈，保证有限次迭代收敛到纳什稳定均衡；室内飞行实验验证了实用性。

Conclusion: 强化学习增强的重叠联盟形成博弈方法有效提高了动态物流任务分配的最优性，在理论收敛性和实际应用性上均表现优越。

Abstract: In dynamic urban logistics, the stochastic emergence of time-sensitive tasks poses a significant optimality challenge for heterogeneous AAVs logistics task allocation. To address this problem, a reinforcement learning enhanced overlapping coalition formation game approach is proposed. A dynamic task allocation model is established, where global optimality is mathematically quantified by a generalized logistics cost coupling service quality and resource consumption. To deal with the time-varying task sets induced by stochastic order arrivals, a transformer-based soft actor-critic network is designed. By leveraging multi-head self-attention to encode variable-length logistics states and capture task-wise spatiotemporal dependencies, the learned policy adaptively guides coalition updates, replacing heuristic rules in the overlapping coalition formation game. On this basis, heterogeneous AAVs can form more efficient overlapping coalitions for dynamic logistics tasks. The resulting coalition formation process is proven to constitute an exact potential game, which guarantees convergence to a Nash-stable equilibrium within a finite number of iterations. Numerical simulations demonstrate that the proposed algorithm effectively improves the optimality of task allocation under the generalized logistics cost criterion. In a scenario with 32 AAVs and 80 tasks, our algorithm achieves a 39.76% cost reduction compared with the heuristic OCF baseline. Indoor flight experiments further validate its practicality.

</details>


### [133] [Efficient On-policy Visual-RL via Stochastic Decoupled Policy Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26478)
*Haoxiang You,Yilang Liu,Davis Zong,Qian Wang,Teeratham Vitchutripop,Qi Wang,Daniel Rakita,Ian Abraham*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SDPG是一种轻量级视觉强化学习方法，通过轨迹滚动的随机扰动估计策略梯度，在单张RTX 4080 GPU上几小时内即可端到端训练多种视觉运动控制策略，大幅降低计算和内存开销，在视觉MuJoCo基准测试中表现优异，并支持真实的视觉机器人基准测试和sim-to-real迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉强化学习方法计算和内存开销大，训练时间过长，难以在单GPU上高效训练。作者希望提出一种轻量级的方法，降低视觉RL的硬件门槛和训练成本。

Method: 提出随机解耦策略梯度（SDPG），通过轨迹滚动的随机扰动来估计策略梯度，无需大量批量渲染环境，显著减少计算和内存开销。

Result: 在视觉MuJoCo基准测试中，SDPG在训练时间、内存使用和奖励方面均优于基线方法。该方法还能在真实机器人硬件上实现有效的sim-to-real迁移。

Conclusion: SDPG是一种高效、轻量的视觉RL方法，能够快速训练多种视觉运动控制策略，并支持真实机器人部署，为未来研究提供了新的基准。

Abstract: We present the stochastic decoupled policy gradient (SDPG), a lightweight visual reinforcement learning (RL) method that trains diverse visuomotor control policies end-to-end within a few hours on a single NVIDIA RTX 4080 GPU. SDPG estimates policy gradients via random perturbations of trajectory rollouts, requiring orders of magnitude fewer batch-rendered environments and substantially reducing compute and memory overhead. On visual MuJoCo benchmarks, SDPG consistently outperforms baseline methods in training time, memory usage, and rewards. Finally, to support future research, we introduce a suite of realistic visual robotics benchmarks spanning dexterous manipulation, challenging locomotion, and demonstrate effective sim-to-real transfer on physical hardware.

</details>


### [134] [Provably Safe Motion Planning Under Unknown Disturbances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26625)
*Ibon Gracia,Qi Heng Ho,Luca Laurenti,Morteza Lahijanian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用轨迹数据学习Wasserstein模糊管的安全采样运动规划算法，为未知分布随机扰动下的机器人系统提供概率完备的安全保证，并通过低维模糊管学习和bandit有效性检查提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以应对未知分布随机扰动下的安全运动规划问题，且传统方法保守性过高、可扩展性差。

Method: 1）从系统轨迹数据中学习Wasserstein模糊管（序列模糊集）；2）基于该模糊管构建概率完备的采样运动规划树；3）采用低维模糊管分解降低保守性；4）设计bandit-based有效性检查器提升计算效率。

Result: 在杂乱环境中能够找到有效路径并满足严格安全阈值，性能优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过数据驱动的Wasserstein模糊管和高效验证策略，实现了对未知分布扰动下的安全运动规划，兼顾理论保证与实际性能。

Abstract: We present a provably safe sampling-based motion planning algorithm for robotic systems affected by random disturbances of unknown distribution. We consider systems with linear or linearizable dynamics evolving in workspace with arbitrary-shaped obstacles subject to state and control constraints. Safety requirements are formulated as chance-constraints. Our approach leverages data from trajectories of the system to learn a Wasserstein ambiguity tube, i.e., a sequence of ambiguity sets, which contains the trajectory of the system's state distribution with high confidence. This ambiguity tube is then used in a probabilistically complete algorithm to grow a sampling-based motion planning tree that respects the constraints of the problem. We show that learning several lower-dimensional ambiguity tubes instead of a single high-dimensional one effectively reduces the conservatism and boosts scalability. Additionally, we design an efficient bandit-based validity checker that remarkably increases the empirical performance of our approach without sacrificing probabilistic completeness. Case studies show our algorithm finds valid plans in cluttered environments under strict safety thresholds, outperforming state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [135] [Enabling Extensible Embodied Capabilities with Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26637)
*Xueyang Zhou,Zijia Wang,Qianjiang Li,Yibo Hu,Guiyao Tie,Li Wan,Yidan Liu,Pan Zhou,Lichao Sun,Yongchao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种能力外部化方法，通过标准化的Embodied Tool Protocol (ETP)将具身智能中的异构能力解耦为独立工具，在EmbodiedToolBench上测试显示工具增强可提升性能（感知和认知类提升显著，执行类提升有限），并揭示了工具调用时机、选择和使用仍是当前模型的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能方法将感知、规划、推理、控制等异构能力统一编码到单一参数化策略中，但这些能力本质上是层次化和异质的，难以可靠学习和模块化。

Method: 提出能力外部化方法，设计Embodied Tool Protocol (ETP)标准化协议，涵盖工具注册、发现、调用和执行，并构建包含100+已验证工具的工具库和EmbodiedToolBench评估基准。

Result: 仿真和真实平台实验表明，能力外部化一致提升具身性能（EB-ALFRED提升31%，EB-Navigation提升36%），认知和感知类增益显著，执行类增益有限。同时发现模型在工具调用时机、选择和方式上仍存在持续挑战。

Conclusion: 能力外部化是提升具身智能的有效方向，但工具使用能力（工具必要性识别、选择、执行与链式组合）仍是关键瓶颈，值得未来深入研究。

Abstract: Most existing embodied intelligence methods formulate perception, reasoning, planning, and control within a unified parameterized policy. Yet these capabilities are inherently hierarchical and heterogeneous, making them difficult to reliably learn and modularize within a single model. We propose a capability externalization approach that decouples heterogeneous capabilities into independently optimized tools, dynamically invoked at inference time. To this end, we introduce Embodied Tool Protocol (ETP), a standardized protocol for embodied tool registration, discovery, invocation, and execution, and curate 100+ validated tools spanning perception, cognition, reasoning, and execution as the tool base. Building on this, we construct EmbodiedToolBench to evaluate both whether tool augmentation improves embodied performance and how well current models use tools across tool-necessity recognition, tool selection, tool execution, and tool-chain composition. Experiments across simulation and real-world platforms confirm that capability externalization consistently improves embodied performance (avg. gain 31% on EB-ALFRED and 36% on EB-Navigation), yet reveal a clear boundary: gains are substantial for cognition and perception but are limited for execution-type capabilities. Moreover, our analysis reveals that knowing when, which, and how to invoke tools remains a persistent challenge across all models, thereby highlighting embodied tool competence as a critical direction for future research.

</details>


### [136] [HyperSim: A Holistic Sim-To-Real Framework For Robust Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26638)
*Junyi Dong,Haotian Luo,Ziwei Xu,Shengwei Bian,Heng Zhang,Sitong Mao,Jingyi Guo,Yang Xu,Wenhao Chen,Qiuyu Feng,Yao Mu,Ping Luo,Shunbo Zhou,Xiaodong Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HyperSim是一个从合成数据生成到策略训练再到真实世界部署的全流程框架，通过高保真环境合成、对抗轨迹生成和仿真-真实联合训练三大模块弥合sim-to-real差距，在400次真实任务执行中取得80%(ACT)和95%(π₀)的成功率，并在物理扰动下将完成率提升35%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升数据规模与多样性对泛化具身智能至关重要，但物理数据采集成本高昂；虽然合成数据生成可扩展，但sim-to-real领域差距严重阻碍了策略从仿真到真实世界的迁移。

Method: HyperSim框架包含三大核心模块：(1)高保真环境合成——提升视觉真实性；(2)对抗轨迹生成——扩展数据覆盖范围；(3)仿真-真实联合训练——强制学习域不变表征，系统性地弥合领域差异。

Result: 在400次真实任务执行中，完整pipeline在ACT和π₀模型上分别达到80%和95%的sim-to-real成功率；使用对抗轨迹训练的策略在物理扰动下完成率提升35%。

Conclusion: HyperSim通过系统性的三模块协同设计，有效弥合了sim-to-real差距，在多种操纵模型和扰动条件下均展现了显著的鲁棒性和部署可行性。

Abstract: Scaling data volume and diversity is critical for generalizing embodied intelligence. While synthetic data generation offers a scalable alternative to expensive physical data acquisition, transferring robotic manipulation policies from simulation to the real world (sim-to-real) remains a formidable challenge due to the domain gap. This paper presents HyperSim, a holistic framework spanning from synthetic data generation to policy training and seamless real-world deployment. To systematically bridge the sim-to-real gap, HyperSim is realized through three core pillars: high-fidelity environment synthesis, adversarial trajectory generation, and sim-and-real co-training. Collectively, these modules address domain discrepancies by enhancing visual fidelity, expanding data coverage, and enforcing domain-invariant representations. We rigorously validate HyperSim through a large-scale empirical study involving 400 real-world task executions across two representative manipulation models. Assessed across three fine-grained metrics, our complete pipeline achieves remarkable sim-to-real success rates of 80% and 95% with ACT and π_{0}, respectively. Furthermore, policies trained on our adversarial trajectories exhibit significantly enhanced robustness against dynamic uncertainties, achieving a 35% higher completion rate under physical perturbations.

</details>


### [137] [L-Learning : A Lyapunov-Based Approach Leveraging Lagrangian Mechanics for Efficient and Stable Robot Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26648)
*Quan Quan,Hao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了L-Learning框架，将李雅普诺夫稳定性理论与拉格朗日力学结合，通过学习系统能量函数实现数据驱动的机器人轨迹跟踪控制，兼顾了控制精度、稳定性保证和样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统控制方法在动态不确定环境中性能下降，而数据驱动方法虽适应性更强，但存在样本复杂度高、缺乏严格稳定性保证的问题。作者旨在弥合两者间的鸿沟。

Method: 将李雅普诺夫稳定性理论与拉格朗日力学融合到数据驱动框架中，显式地从数据中学习系统的能量函数，在优化控制性能的同时内在保证闭环稳定性。

Result: L-Learning具有优越的控制精度、理论上的稳定性保证以及高样本效率，在动态不确定环境中表现出色。

Conclusion: L-Learning是兼顾稳定性与性能的数据驱动控制方案，为实际机器人应用提供了有前景的解决方案，有效克服了传统方法和纯数据驱动方法各自的局限性。

Abstract: This paper presents L-Learning, a novel data-driven control framework for robotics that integrates Lyapunov stability theory with Lagrangian mechanics to enhance trajectory tracking performance. While traditional control methods often suffer from performance degradation in dynamic and uncertain environments, data-driven approaches, while more adaptable, are frequently limited by high sample complexity and a lack of rigorous stability guarantees. L-Learning mitigates these challenges by explicitly learning the system's energy function from data, thereby optimizing performance while ensuring closed-loop stability intrinsically. Characterized by superior control accuracy, theoretical stability guarantees, and high sample efficiency, L-Learning represents a promising solution for practical robotic applications.

</details>


### [138] [On the Generalization Capabilities, Design Choices and Limitations of Keypoint Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26649)
*Thomas Lips,Marco Moletta,Michael C. Welle,Danica Kragic,Francis wyffels*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究基于视觉基础模型的关键点模仿学习（KIL）在机器人操作中的泛化能力，通过2000+次真实世界实验验证，KIL在5个任务上达到75%成功率，优于RGB基线（47%），与S2-diffusion（73%）相当，但并未超越替代表示方法，且继承了基础模型的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RGB模仿学习需要大量演示才能泛化到未见物体或场景，因此需要探索能够提升泛化能力的中间表示方法。

Method: 结合前人关键点模仿学习（KIL）工作，利用视觉基础模型进行一次性关键点提取，并通过2000+真实世界rollouts评估多种设计选择。

Result: KIL在5个任务上总体成功率为75%，显著高于RGB基线47%，与S2-diffusion的73%持平。但KIL并未超越替代表示方法。

Conclusion: KIL是一种数据高效的机器人学习方法，但受限于所用基础模型的能力，且并不优于其他表示方法。研究提供了实践指南，并扩展至多物体实例任务。

Abstract: RGB-based imitation learning requires many demonstrations to generalize to unseen objects or scenes, motivating research into intermediate representations to improve generalization for robotic manipulation. Visual foundation models enable one-shot extraction of keypoints to provide such representation. However, it remains unclear how to integrate them into imitation learning optimally and when they outperform alternative representations. We combine approaches from previous works on keypoint imitation learning (KIL) and investigate several design choices to provide practical guidelines. Using over 2000 real-world rollouts, we also assess the generalization capabilities of KIL to unseen objects and scene variations. KIL achieves a 75% overall success rate across five tasks, significantly outperforming the RGB baseline (47%) and performing on par with S2-diffusion (73%). Finally, we explore the limitations of the foundation models used for keypoint extraction and extend KIL to tasks with multiple object instances. Our results confirm KIL as a data-efficient approach for robot learning, though it does not outperform alternative representations and inherits limitations of the foundation models used for keypoint extraction. All rollout videos, demonstrations, and results are available at https://kil-manipulation.github.io/.

</details>


### [139] [SteelDS: A High-Resolution Video Dataset of E40 Steel Scrap for Object Detection and Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26682)
*Melanie Neubauer,Christian Rauch,Gerald Koinig,Alexia Tischberger-Aldrian,Roland Pomberger,Elmar Rueckert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文介绍了一个包含24,297帧高分辨率标注视频的数据集，记录了传送带上粉碎的E40级钢和铜废料，用于支持机器学习模型在材料分类、目标检测和实例分割方面的开发，作为评估自动化分拣算法识别铜杂质的基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业磁选后阶段通常需要人工干预去除铜杂质，缺乏高质量标注数据集来训练自动化分拣算法，因此需要构建一个能反映真实工业分拣条件的基准数据集。

Method: 在受控实验室环境中捕捉传送带上的钢和铜废料视频，提供包括像素级分割掩码和材料类别在内的标注，涵盖5个子集、396个钢和101个铜物体，并引入物体间距和密度变化以模拟真实条件。

Result: 生成了包含24,297帧标注帧的数据集，按尺寸分类的396个钢和101个铜物体，提供了像素级分割掩码和材料类别等标注。

Conclusion: 该数据集可作为评估自动化分拣算法识别复杂异质废钢流中铜杂质的基准，支持材料分类、目标检测和实例分割等机器学习任务。

Abstract: This dataset provides high-resolution, annotated video sequences of shredded E40-grade steel and copper scrap on a conveyor belt. Captured in a controlled laboratory environment, the data reflects the industrial post-magnetic sorting stage, where manual intervention is typically required to remove copper contaminants. The dataset comprises 24,297 labeled frames across five subsets, featuring 396 steel and 101 copper objects categorized by size. It supports the development of machine learning models for material classification, object detection, and instance segmentation. Variations in object spacing and density are included to simulate realistic industrial sorting conditions. Ground truth annotations include pixel-wise segmentation masks and material classes. This dataset serves as a benchmark for evaluating automated sorting algorithms aiming to identify copper impurities within complex, heterogeneous steel scrap streams.

</details>


### [140] [Look Further: Socially-Compliant Navigation System in Residential Buildings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26710)
*Akira Shiba,Marina Obata,Nathan Kau,Zoltan Beck,Rishi Shah,Michael Sudano,Sabrina Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究移动机器人在住宅走廊环境中，将反应距离延长至8米以上（远超通常的人际交互距离），通过引入主动变道（PLC）运动模式来改善人类对机器人运动的感知。用户研究表明，在直线走廊场景中，该方法在安全性、流畅性和礼貌性方面显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统社交导航方法通常聚焦于近距离（数米内）的人机交互避让，但作者认为通过将反应距离大幅延伸至8米以上，可以进一步改善人类对机器人运动的感知，从而提升交互质量。

Method: 提出主动变道（Proactive Lane-Changing, PLC）运动模式，即机器人在走廊中遇到前方行人时，在8米距离处即从中心位置横向移动至一侧。并设计了相应的导航系统来实现该模式。

Result: 在直线走廊（正面接近）场景中，PLC方法在安全性、流畅性和礼貌性三个服务目标上均显著优于减速、停止和近距离反应式避让等传统方法。但在路口（盲角）场景中，各方法无显著差异，参与者偏好多样。

Conclusion: 延长机器人的反应距离（至8米以上）可以有效提升用户在直线走廊场景中对机器人运动的正面感知；但在复杂场景（如盲角路口）中，单一运动模式难以满足所有用户的偏好，需要更个性化的设计。

Abstract: The distance at which a mobile robot reacts to a person strongly impacts various qualities of the human-robot interaction. In this paper, we focus on the navigation of a mobile delivery robot platform in a residential indoor hallway environment. Social navigation methods typically focus on avoiding uncomfortable human-robot interactions, such as when a robot encroaches on someone's personal space. Since personal space has been shown to be in the range of just a few meters, social navigation methods typically focus on deconflicting and resolving these short-range interactions. In this work, however, we demonstrate that by extending the reaction distance to over eight meters, far beyond the typical interaction distance, we can improve the human's perception of the robot's motion. We introduce the Proactive Lane-Changing (PLC) motion pattern and a navigation system that leverages it to react to people at an increased distance. This pattern consists of changing the robot's lateral position as it navigates down the hallway from the center to the side at an eight-meter distance from an oncoming person.
  We conducted a user study with 42 participants to assess their impressions of the delivery robot based on three service objectives: safety, smoothness, and politeness. In the straight hallway scenario (Frontal Approach), results showed significant improvement in each of these three objectives compared to typical motion patterns found in the literature: slowing down, stopping, and reactive collision avoidance in the proximity of a person. In contrast, in the intersection (Blind Corner) scenarios, none of the approaches performed significantly better than any other, with participants having a diverse range of preferences among robot motion patterns.

</details>


### [141] [Manipulating Tangible Virtual Object Dynamics to Promote Learning of Precision Force Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26782)
*Alberto Garzás-Villar,Alba Riera-Cardona,Alexis Derumigny,J. Micah Prendergast,Jane Murray Cramm,Laura Marchal-Crespo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出利用虚拟现实中的非线性弹性体动力学（AS-高斯函数）来训练精细力控制，实验表明该方法在训练中优于线性弹簧模型，但长期保留效果无显著差异，且参与者主要依赖目标伸长量而非目标力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人触觉设备结合虚拟现实为训练精细力控制提供了新机会，而精细力控制是卒中后康复中一个重要但被忽视的组成部分。本文旨在通过操控虚拟物体的渲染动力学来训练精确力控制，同时激活体感系统。

Method: 50名健康参与者进行模拟冰壶任务，需拉伸虚拟弹簧产生目标释放力将冰壶推送至指定位置。训练中弹簧力-伸长关系分别建模为线性函数、高斯函数或反对称高斯（AS-高斯）函数（在目标释放力处导数为零）。分析训练表现、个性特征及长期保留效果。

Result: AS-高斯组在训练中始终比线性组获得更高的力精度；高斯组仅在训练末期优于线性组。高Free Spirit得分者在高斯动力学下表现更差且探索减少，而高Transform-of-Challenge得分与更多探索相关。三种弹簧类型的长期保留无显著差异。参与者主要依赖目标伸长量而非目标力。

Conclusion: 非线性弹性体动力学在训练精细力控制方面有潜力，但方法需要改进以减少对本体感觉线索的依赖，才能用于神经系统患者的康复测试。

Abstract: Robotic haptic devices combined with virtual reality offer novel opportunities to train fine force generation, an essential yet overlooked component of post-stroke rehabilitation. This study proposes that manipulating the rendered dynamics of tangible virtual objects can be leveraged to train precise force control while engaging the somatosensory system. We conducted an experiment with fifty healthy participants who performed a curling-inspired task in which they had to stretch a virtual spring to generate a target release force to propel the stone to a predefined location on the ice sheet. During training, the spring's force-elongation relationship was modeled as either a linear or non-linear function, i.e., a Gaussian or antisymmetric Gaussian (AS-Gaussian) function with zero derivative at the release target force. Results indicate that the AS-Gaussian group consistently achieved higher force accuracy during training than the linear group, while the Gaussian group only outperformed the linear group toward the end of training. Analysis of personality traits revealed that higher Free Spirit scores were associated with poorer performance and reduced task exploration under Gaussian dynamics, whereas higher Transform-of-Challenge scores correlated with increased exploration. Despite these training effects, no significant differences in long-term retention were found across spring types or personality traits. Participants primarily relied on learned target elongation rather than target force, as evidenced by performance in a transfer task with a different stiffness but the same target force. While promising for somatosensory neurorehabilitation, these methods require refinement to reduce reliance on proprioceptive cues before testing with neurological patients.

</details>


### [142] [Can VLA Models Learn from Real-World Data Continually without Forgetting?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26820)
*Jiarun Zhu,Yijun Hong,Xiaoquan Sun,Zetian Xu,Mingqi Yuan,Zhiyong Wang,Wenjun Zeng,Jiayu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次实证研究了真实场景下VLA模型的持续学习问题，发现存在严重灾难性遗忘，并系统评估了经验回放方法的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在通用机器人领域前景广阔，但真实场景中需要持续学习新技能并保留旧知识，现有研究仅在模拟环境中探索，缺乏真实条件下的分析。

Method: 构建了一个包含四项连续操作任务（刚体抓取放置、接触压、可变形折叠）的真实世界持续学习数据集，进行综合实验并系统评估经验回放方法。

Result: VLA模型在真实异构演示数据持续学习时存在严重灾难性遗忘；识别出经验回放方法成功的关键实现因素。

Conclusion: 该工作为真实世界VLA持续学习提供了首次实证研究，为部署长期机器人策略提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models provide a promising foundation for general-purpose robotics. However, their successful deployment in real-world scenarios requires the ability to continually acquire new skills while retaining previously learned behaviors. While pioneering research has studied the continual learning of VLA models in narrowly simulated environments, this challenge remains largely unexplored under realistic conditions. To address this limitation, we construct a real-world continual learning dataset comprising four sequential manipulation tasks, spanning rigid-object pick-and-place, contact-rich pressing, and deformable-object folding. Using this dataset, we conduct comprehensive experiments and find that VLA models suffer significant catastrophic forgetting when continually learning from heterogeneous real-world demonstrations. We then systematically evaluate experience replay and uncover key implementation factors that govern its success. In summary, this work provides the first empirical study of real-world continual VLA learning and offers practical guidance for deploying long-lived robot policies.

</details>


### [143] [Learning Compositional Symbolic Task Rules from Demonstrations with Inductive Logic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26828)
*Oleh Borys,Karla Stepanova*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出用归纳逻辑编程（ILP）将复杂任务分解为不同抽象层次的学习目标，从演示中学习可解释、可复用的符号规则，并在块组装任务中验证了方法的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习不仅需要捕获任务的执行方式，还需要理解任务的高层结构，且表示需具备可检查性、可复用性和人类可解释性。

Method: 利用归纳逻辑编程将复杂任务分解为不同本体抽象层次的多个简单学习目标，从演示和领域知识中推断符号规则，并在学习高层结构时复用已学规则。

Result: 在合成块组装场景中，学习到的抽象具有可解释性，并能在包含未见物体的更难任务上实现强泛化。

Conclusion: 分解式ILP是实现任务级模仿学习的一种可行方法。

Abstract: Learning from Demonstration~(LfD) should capture not only how a task is executed, but also its high-level task structure that explains the demonstrated behavior. As robots become more autonomous, such task representations must be inspectable, reusable, and human-interpretable. To address this, we study how to represent and learn robotic tasks with inductive logic programming~(ILP) by decomposing a complex task into a series of simpler learning objectives at different abstraction (ontological) levels. The system infers symbolic rules from demonstrations and prior (domain) knowledge, and reuses learned rules when learning higher-level task structure. We evaluate the approach in a synthetic block-assembly scenario and show that the learned abstractions are interpretable and support strong generalization to harder, held-out tasks with unseen objects. These results provide preliminary evidence that decomposed ILP is a feasible approach to task-level LfD.

</details>


### [144] [A Bioinspired Underwater Robot with a Latch-Mediated Soft Bistable Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26936)
*Chongze Bi,Wenjie Wu,Zonghao Zuo,Li Wen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仿生软体双稳态驱动器，集成闩锁机制，利用单个电机实现非对称能量储存与释放，结合鳍状结构，使小型水下机器人能高效推进与灵活转向，最大推力0.528N，冲量0.147Ns，垂直位移30mm，可完成垂直上升、斜向前进和侧向平移等多种运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统小型水下机器人受限于低能量密度的电源，而自然界如螳螂虾和跳蚤利用闩锁-弹簧驱动（LaMSA）系统实现了快速运动。作者希望复现这种快速、非对称的驱动方式于简单紧凑的结构中，以突破小型水下机器人的动力瓶颈。

Method: 设计了一种仿生软体双稳态驱动器，集成闩锁机构，仅需单个电机即可实现非对称的能量输入与释放；并耦合鳍状结构实现水下推进与机动。

Result: 实验实现了稳定的周期性拍动、精确转向；最大推力0.528N，冲量0.147Ns，垂直位移30mm；通过调节鳍角可实现垂直上升、斜向前进和侧向平移。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种新颖、节能的小型水下机器人运动控制方案，为探索、环境监测和巡检等领域的先进仿生设计铺平了道路。

Abstract: Underwater robotics has advanced significantly over recent decades. however, the development of miniaturized underwater robots remains limited by low energy densities of traditional power sources. Nature offers compelling solutions-organisms like mantis shrimps and fleas utilize latch-mediated spring actuation (LaMSA) systems that achieve rapid movements through a decoupled energy storage and release mechanism. Despite extensive studies of LaMSA, replicating such rapid, asymmetric actuation within simple, compact structures remains challenging. In this work, we introduce a bioinspired, soft bistable actuator with an integrated latch mechanism that enables asymmetric energy input and release using a single motor. Coupled with fin structures, this design facilitates efficient underwater propulsion and maneuverability. Experimental results demonstrate stable periodic flapping, precise steering, and a maximum thrust of 0.528 N, impulse of 0.147 Ns, and vertical displacement of 30 mm. By modulating fin angles, the robot achieves versatile motions, including vertical ascent, diagonal forward movement, and lateral translation. This study presents a novel, energy-efficient approach for controlling motion in compact underwater robots, paving the way for advanced biomimetic designs with potential applications in exploration, environmental monitoring, and inspection.

</details>


### [145] [Object Pose and Shape Estimation for Grasping: Does it Work?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26944)
*Pavan Karke,Kushal Shah,Gaurav Singh,Md Faizal Karim,K Madhava Krishna,Rajat Talak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了模块化的物体姿态与形状估计方法（结合对极抓取采样）是否优于端到端抓取合成方法，实验表明模块化方法在所有测试中均表现更优，但性能依赖于姿态与形状估计的精度，且在杂乱场景中会有所退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究当前物体姿态与形状估计方法是否足够成熟，能否在结合对极抓取采样后超越端到端抓取合成方法，以明确模块化方法的实际应用价值。

Method: 将一种端到端抓取合成方法与三种模块化方法进行比较。模块化方法先通过编码器-解码器模型或扩散模型估计场景中所有物体的姿态与形状，再使用对极采样生成7-DoF的平行夹爪抓取，输入为单视图RGB-D图像。

Result: 模块化方法在所有实验中均优于端到端方法，尤其对小物体能生成大量抓取，而端到端方法在此类场景中失败。但模块化方法的有效性依赖于姿态与形状估计的精度，在杂乱场景中性能部分退化。此外，模块化方法可结合视觉语言模型实现语言条件抓取，性能与LERV-TOGO基线相当。

Conclusion: 模块化的姿态与形状估计+对极抓取采样在单视图RGB-D输入下优于端到端抓取合成方法，但现有姿态与形状估计方法在杂乱场景中的局限性仍需改进。

Abstract: The problem of object pose and shape estimation has seen key advancements lately. Encoder-decoder (e.g., SAM3D, LRM, CRISP) and diffusion-based models (e.g., InstantMesh, Zero123, SceneComplete) have shown category-agnostic shape encoding capacity and open-set generalizability. In this work, we ask the question: Are the object pose and shape estimation methods mature enough, such that when used with antipodal grasp sampling, can outperform the end-to-end grasp synthesis methods? We explore this question in detail by scoping our study to parallel jaw grippers, 7-DoF grasps, and single-view RGB(-D) image as input. We implement and compare a state-of-the-art, end-to-end grasp synthesis method and three modular methods, which first estimate the object pose and shape for all objects in the scene, and generate grasps using antipodal sampling. We observe that the modular methods outperform the end-to-end method in all our experiments. The modular methods are able to synthesize plenty of grasps, even for small objects, where the end-to-end methods fail. The effectiveness of the modular methods is contingent on the accuracy of the pose and shape estimation, and suffers partial degradation in cluttered scenes - a limitation of the existing pose and shape estimation methods. We also analyze the failure modes and run-times for the three modular methods, which use two different ways of object pose and shape estimation: one based on an encoder-decoder model, while another a diffusion model. Finally, we demonstrate that the single-view object pose and shape estimation methods can be augmented with vision-language models to yield language-conditioned grasps from just single-view RGB-D image as input. We notice comparable performance to the state-of-the-art LERF-TOGO baseline.

</details>


### [146] [Trust, Geometry, and Rules: A Credibility-Aware Reinforcement Learning Framework for Safe USV Navigation under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26974)
*Yuhang Zhang,Shuqi Chai,Yukang Zhang,Liusha Yang,Mingchuan Zhang,Wei Wang,Qingjiang Shi,Quanbo Ge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种融合可信度感知学习、几何安全防护和连续规则感知嵌入的框架，用于解决USV在动态海洋环境中感知不确定时的自主导航和COLREGs合规问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL方法因状态估计误差导致不稳定的信念状态，以及离散交通规则导致学习目标不连续，难以在感知系统存在校准不确定性时实现安全合规的自主导航。

Method: 提出三个核心组件：(1) CW-VL：利用滤波器估计协方差与经验误差的差异动态调整评论家损失权重；(2) CI-VO：将位置估计不确定性映射为角度边界，形成几何安全防护罩；(3) Risk-Aware COLREGs Duty Embedding：将二元职责松弛为连续信号，抑制稀疏奖励导致的振荡。

Result: 仿真实验表明，该方法在感知不一致的情况下具有更强的训练鲁棒性，且在碰撞规避和COLREGs合规方面优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过可信度加权学习、几何安全防护和连续规则嵌入三个机制的有效结合，显著提升了USV在动态海事环境中感知不确定性下的自主导航安全性和规则合规性。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation of Unmanned Surface Vehicles (USVs) that is safe and compliant with the International Regulations for Preventing Collisions at Sea (COLREGs) remains a formidable challenge in dynamic maritime environments, particularly when perception systems exhibit miscalibrated uncertainty. Existing Reinforcement Learning (RL)-based methods often falter because state-estimation errors induce unreliable belief states that mislead the value function, while discrete traffic rules introduce discontinuity in the learning objective. To address these challenges, we propose a framework integrating credibility-aware learning, geometric safety shielding, and continuous rule-aware embedding. First, Credibility-Weighted Value Learning (CW-VL) introduces a dynamic trust factor derived from the discrepancy between filter-estimated covariance and empirical error statistics to modulate the critic's heteroscedastic loss, preventing policy overfitting to noisy samples. Second, the Covariance-Inflated Velocity Obstacle (CI-VO) maps position-estimation uncertainty into set-wise angular margins, forming a conservative geometric shield that overrides hazardous exploratory actions. Third, Risk-Aware COLREGs Duty Embedding relaxes binary encounter duties into continuous rule-aware signals, providing smooth sector-transition information and suppressing oscillation from sparse rule rewards. Simulated encounter studies demonstrate improved training robustness against perceptual inconsistency and superior collision avoidance and COLREGs compliance over baselines.

</details>


### [147] [Towards Shared Embodied Intelligence in Humanoid Robots through Optimization Development and Testing of the Human Aware ergoCub Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26991)
*Carlotta Sartore,Mohamed Elobaid,Lorenzo Rapetti,Giulio Romualdi,Stefano Dafarra,Nicola A. Piga,Ines Sorrentino,Paolo Maria Vicecone,Silvio Traversaro,Ugo Pattacini,Luca Fiorio,Francesco Draicchio,Giovanna Tranfo,Lorenzo Natale,Marco Maggiali,Daniele Pucci*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种融合共享智能与具身认知的人形机器人架构，通过优化硬件与物理智能参数以匹配人体工效学指标，实现人机安全协作，并给出具体实现——ergoCub机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类通过共享智能协调行动，且身体与认知能力随环境优化（具身认知），但现有机器人设计缺乏将这两种原则统一于人形机器人以实现安全有效协作的系统框架。

Method: 提出一个集成架构，将人机交互建模为硬件配置的函数，并将人体模型嵌入机器人的物理智能中；通过优化机器人硬件和物理智能参数来匹配人体工效学指标。

Result: 实现了人形机器人ergoCub，其形态与控制针对人类协作任务进行了优化，在硬件和物理智能层面均优先考虑了人体工效学。

Conclusion: 该框架为设计以人机工效为核心的人形机器人提供了系统性方法，可应用于工业与辅助机器人领域。

Abstract: Collaboration is central to human behavior, enabling tasks beyond individual capability. This ability arises from coordinating actions through internal representations of others, a concept known as shared intelligence. Additionally, humans are characterized by physical bodies and cognitive abilities that are optimized in response to their environment, a phenomenon referred to as embodied cognition. Designing humanoid robots that collaborate safely and effectively with people requires unifying these principles. Here we propose an architecture that integrates shared intelligence and embodied cognition to enable robots to physically collaborate with humans, where robot hardware and control are optimized for human metrics, using representations of the human body and motion intelligence. The ultimate goal is to achieve a form of shared embodied intelligence. Specifically, our architecture optimizes robot hardware and physical intelligence parameters with respect to human ergonomic metrics. This is accomplished by modeling human-robot interaction as a function of hardware configurations and embedding human models into the robot's physical intelligence. As a concrete implementation, we present the humanoid robot ergoCub, whose morphology and control have been optimized for collaborative tasks with humans. Our approach provides a framework for designing humanoid robots that prioritize human ergonomics at both the hardware and physical intelligence levels, with applications in industrial and assistive robotics.

</details>


### [148] [TPS-Drive: Task-Guided Representation Purification for VLM-based Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27038)
*Jiaxiang Li,Yumao Liu,Ke Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TPS-Drive框架，通过任务引导的表示净化（Task-Guided Representation Purification）解决VLM在自动驾驶规划中的空间幻觉和表示干扰问题，核心是Agent-Centric Tokenizer（基于任务引导的向量量化机制）和分离式推理流程，在nuScenes、NAVSIMv1/v2基准上实现了更优的空间预测和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM自动驾驶方法存在两类问题：文本对齐方法将连续空间状态压扁为符号，破坏几何结构导致"空间幻觉"；密集视觉方法保留空间拓扑但背景纹理冗余干扰表示。两者均无法有效结合语义推理与精确3D空间预测。

Method: 1) Agent-Centric Tokenizer：利用冻结的3D检测头监督的任务引导向量量化机制，将有限码本容量从静态背景重新分配到动态智能体，隔离空间冗余；2) 解耦推理流程：依次执行场景理解→未来预测→动作生成；3) 三阶段渐进训练范式，最终用奖励驱动优化超越纯模仿学习。

Result: 在开环nuScenes上实现精准智能体空间状态预测并降低碰撞率；在闭环NAVSIMv1和NAVSIMv2基准上创下新的安全记录。

Conclusion: 任务引导的表示净化策略能有效桥接VLM的语义推理与3D空间预测，Agent-Centric Tokenizer通过码本重分配解决背景干扰，为自动驾驶安全规划提供了更可靠的范式。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) provide a promising foundation for autonomous driving planning, yet bridging semantic reasoning and precise 3D spatial forecasting remains a critical challenge. Existing representation strategies generally follow two paths: text-aligned methods flatten continuous spatial states into symbols, which compromises geometric structure and induces "spatial hallucinations"; dense visual methods preserve spatial topology but overwhelm standard tokenizers with redundant background textures, leading to "representation interference". To address these limitations, we introduce TPS-Drive, a novel framework centered on Task-Guided Representation Purification that empowers VLMs to Think in Purified Space. At its core, an Agent-Centric Tokenizer utilizes a task-guided vector quantization mechanism supervised by a frozen 3D detection head, which explicitly reallocates limited codebook capacity from pervasive static backgrounds to critical dynamic agents and effectively isolates spatial redundancy. Leveraging this purified spatial vocabulary, TPS-Drive employs a decoupled reasoning pipeline that sequentially performs scene understanding, future forecasting, and action generation. The framework is optimized via a progressive three-stage training paradigm, culminating in reward-driven refinement that surpasses pure imitation learning. Extensive experiments validate our approach: TPS-Drive achieves accurate agent spatial state forecasting and reduces collision rates in open-loop nuScenes evaluations, while establishing new safety records on the rigorous closed-loop NAVSIMv1 and NAVSIMv2 benchmarks.

</details>


### [149] [Learning to Balance Motor Thermal Safety and Quadrupedal Locomotion Performance with Residual Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27046)
*Yuhang Wan,Weixian Lin,Letian Qian,Yiqi Zou,Weiwei Wu,Shengwei Wu,Chuanlin Zhao,Xin Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段强化学习训练框架，将四足机器人的全身热模型集成到训练流程中，通过残差策略在标称步态策略基础上根据电机温度状态进行修正，有效平衡电机热安全与运动性能。在Unitree A1机器人上，负载3kg时实现了超过13分钟的多地形稳定运动，而标称策略仅能维持约5分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电机热管理在电驱动机器人（尤其是足式机器人）中常被忽视，但电机过热是限制长时运动（尤其在负载条件下）的关键因素。

Method: 1) 将四足机器人全身热模型集成到强化学习流程中，用于更新电机温度。2) 提出两阶段训练框架：先预训练标称策略作为基础运动基线，再训练残差策略基于热状态提供修正动作。

Result: 仿真和真实实验均验证了方法的有效性。真实实验中，Unitree A1在3kg负载下能在多种地形上稳定运动超过13分钟，而标称策略约5分钟即过热。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效平衡了电机热安全与运动性能，显著延长了机器人的负载续航时间，为解决足式机器人热管理问题提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Motor thermal management is often overlooked in the context of electrically-actuated robots, particularly legged robots, but motor overheating is a key factor that limits long-duration locomotion especially under payload conditions.
