This paper integrates a whole-body thermal model of a quadruped robot into the reinforcement learning pipeline to update motor temperatures, and proposes a two-stage training framework for motor thermal management. In this framework, a nominal policy is first pre-trained as a locomotion baseline capable of traversing diverse terrains. A residual policy is then trained on top of the nominal policy to provide corrective actions based on the robot's thermal state, ensuring high performance under low-temperature conditions and preventing motor overheating under high-temperature conditions. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed policy achieves an effective balance between motor thermal safety and locomotion performance. Real-world experiments on a Unitree A1 quadruped robot further validate the approach: under a 3 kg payload, the robot achieves stable locomotion across multiple terrains for over 13 minutes, while the nominal policy alone leads to motor overheating in about 5 minutes.

</details>


### [150] [VR-DAgger: Immersive VR for Dexterous Data Collection and Uncertainty-Guided On-Policy Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27114)
*René Zurbrügg,Tifanny Portela,Arjun Bhardwaj,Aravind Elanjimattathil Vijayan,Maximum Wilder-Smith,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文指出模仿学习中的分布偏移导致小误差累积、性能下降，专家常需处理冗余修正而非关键失败案例，因此需要更高效的数据收集方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人模仿学习面临任务特定数据收集的瓶颈，尤其是分布偏移下性能退化严重，专家时间被低效消耗在冗余修正上。

Method: 聚焦于识别和优先处理关键失败案例，减少低价值冗余修正，提升数据收集效率。

Result: 通过减少冗余专家修正，可缓解分布偏移带来的性能退化问题。

Conclusion: 需要更智能的数据收集策略，优先关注关键失败场景，以突破模仿学习的数据瓶颈。

Abstract: Learning from demonstrations is effective for robotic manipulation, but collecting sufficient task-specific data remains a major bottleneck. Under distribution shift, small errors compound, performance degrades, and expert time is often spent on redundant, low-value corrections instead of the few critical failure cases.

</details>


### [151] [TCBiRRT: Rapid Motion Planning for Tightly Coupled Dual-arm Space Manipulator Using Task-space Random Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27167)
*Jiawei Zhang,Xinhao Miao,Jifeng Guo,Qinghua Li,Chengchao Bai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TCBiRRT算法，通过在任务空间进行随机采样和节点扩展，结合双向RRT框架和重抓取机制，有效解决了闭环约束下双机械臂在复杂环境中的运动规划问题，显著提升了成功率和规划效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧耦合双臂空间机械臂在闭环约束下的运动规划问题：闭环约束大幅缩减了可行配置空间，现有规划器难以在杂乱环境中高效生成无碰撞运动。

Method: 提出TCBiRRT算法：在任务空间（被操作物体位姿）进行随机采样和节点扩展，通过路径逆运动学映射到关节空间，结合双向RRT框架和重抓取机制连接两棵随机树。

Result: 在多种复杂度的在轨组装场景中，TCBiRRT相比现有规划器显著提高了成功率，规划时间提升数个数量级。

Conclusion: 提出的方法为紧耦合双臂空间机械臂的运动规划提供了一种高效且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Planning the motion path for a tightly coupled dual-arm space manipulator under closed-chain constraints is a fundamental yet challenging problem in on-orbit assembly of large-scale space structures. The closed-chain constraints significantly reduce the feasible configuration space, making it difficult for existing planners to efficiently generate collision-free motions, especially in cluttered environments. To address this issue, this paper proposes a task-space constrained bidirectional rapidly-exploring random tree algorithm, termed TCBiRRT. Unlike conventional methods that operate in the high-dimensional configuration space, the proposed approach performs random sampling and node expansion directly in the task space defined by the manipulated object pose. A task-space node expansion strategy is developed to generate candidate object motions, which are then mapped to continuous joint paths using a path inverse kinematics algorithm. The method is further integrated with a bidirectional RRT framework and a regrasp mechanism to efficiently connect two random trees. Extensive simulations are conducted in representative on-orbit assembly scenarios with varying levels of environmental complexity. The results demonstrate that TCBiRRT achieves significantly higher success rates and orders-of-magnitude improvements in planning time compared to state-of-the-art planners. The proposed method provides an efficient and robust solution for motion planning of tightly coupled dual-arm space manipulators.

</details>


### [152] [Towards Drone-based Mapping of Volcanic Gases using Gas Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27180)
*Marius Schaab,Niklas Karbach,Antonia Rabe,Thomas Wiedemann,Patrick Hinsen,Dmitriy Shutin,Thorsten Hoffmann,Achim J. Lilienthal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模型的无人机气体层析成像方法，通过开放路径遥感技术克服了无人机旋翼下洗流对火山气体检测的干扰，并在泥火山现场成功实现了CO2排放的精确测绘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 火山排放大量CO2影响人类生活，传统无人机原位传感器在检测火山气体时受旋翼下洗流干扰导致气体 plume 被吹散，亟需解决这一问题以实现安全的火山气体监测。

Method: 采用无人机搭载开放路径遥感传感器进行气体层析成像，并结合拉格朗日模型补偿风引起的平流效应，实现基于模型的气体断层重建。

Result: 在Salinelle dei Cappuccini泥火山现场实验中，无人机原位传感器因气动扰动未能检测到CO2，而开放路径遥感成功获取气体分布；重建的气体分布图与人工采集的原位测量结果一致。

Conclusion: 基于模型的气体层析成像有效克服了无人机下洗流限制，能够准确测绘火山排放，为火山监测和气候环境影响评估提供了可靠技术手段。

Abstract: Volcanoes emit large amounts of CO2, directly influencing human lives. Mapping volcanic gas emissions helps to forecast eruptions and understand the impact of volcanoes on climate and the environment. Drone-based gas sensing significantly reduces risks in volcanic monitoring but faces technical limitations when measuring gas, as rotor downwash disperses the gas plume before detection. Gas Tomography using remote gas sensing addresses this challenge. At the Salinelle dei Cappuccini mud volcanoes, we demonstrate that while drone-mounted in-situ sensors failed to detect CO2 emissions due to aerodynamic disturbance, open-path sensing successfully enabled remote gas distribution mapping. We present a novel model-based gas tomographic reconstruction approach that incorporates a Lagrangian model to compensate for wind-induced advection. The resulting gas distribution maps align with manually collected in-situ measurements, confirming that model-based gas tomography effectively overcomes downwash limitations and enables accurate mapping of volcanic emissions.

</details>


### [153] [FineVLA: Fine-Grained Instruction Alignment for Steerable Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27284)
*Xintong Hu,Xuhong Huang,Jinyu Zhang,Yutong Yao,Yuchong Sun,Qiuyue Wang,Mingsheng Li,Sicheng Xie,Yitao Liu,Junhao Chen,Yixuan Chen,Yingming Zheng,Shuai Bai,Tao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FineVLA 提出了一种面向视觉-语言-动作模型的动作对齐细粒度监督框架，通过构建包含47,159条细粒度轨迹的数据集、专用基准和可引导策略，证明细粒度语言指令（如何执行）能与目标级指令（执行什么）互补，显著提升机器人任务成功率与可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人数据集通常只配以粗粒度的目标级语言描述，缺乏执行细节（如活动手臂、接近方向、接触区域等），这限制了策略的可引导学习与机器人视频理解能力。

Method: (1) 构建了统一10个开源数据集、包含972,247条轨迹和85K任务的FineVLA-Data（经人工验证的47,159条细粒度轨迹）；(2) 设计了包含500个视频、10,816个原子事实和1,030个VQA问题的留存基准；(3) 开发了机器人专用的VLM标注器用于可扩展的细粒度标注；(4) 训练了可控混合细粒度与原始目标级指令的VLA策略。

Result: 细粒度监督相比纯原始指令成功率高1.4~8.1个百分点；细粒度与原始指令呈互补关系，最佳混合比例（FG:Raw=1:2至1:1）下仿真任务达86.8%/82.5%，真实双臂操作达62.7/100（纯原始指令仅49.9）；真实场景在姿态、颜色和接近方向上的增益最大（+23、+18、+18）。

Conclusion: 细粒度语言应作为目标级指令的补充，即在指定"做什么"的同时明确"如何做"，两者结合能显著提升VLA策略的性能与可引导性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are increasingly expected to not only complete robot tasks, but also follow human instructions about how those tasks should be executed. However, existing robot datasets usually pair trajectories with coarse goal-level language, leaving execution-critical details such as active arm, approach direction, and contact region unspecified. This limits steerable policy learning and robotic video understanding. We introduce FineVLA, an open framework for action-aligned fine-grained VLA supervision. The framework includes: (1) a data construction tool that unifies 972,247 trajectories across 85K tasks from 10 open-source robot datasets and builds FineVLA-Data, a human-verified dataset of 47,159 fine-grained trajectories; (2) a held-out benchmark with 500 videos, 10,816 atomic facts, and 1,030 VQA questions; (3) a robotics-specialized VLM annotator for scalable fine-grained annotation; and (4) a steerable VLA policy trained with controlled mixtures of fine-grained and raw goal-level instructions. Our experiments yield three findings. First, fine-grained supervision does not sacrifice goal-level success: FG-only improves over Raw-only by +1.4 to +8.1 success-rate points across settings. Second, fine-grained and raw instructions are complementary, following a consistent inverted-U trend peaking at FG:Raw = 1:2 to 1:1. The best mixed setting reaches 86.8%/82.5% in RoboTwin simulation and 62.7/100 in real-world dual-arm manipulation (vs. 49.9 Raw-only). Third, fine-grained supervision improves steerable control: the largest real-world gains appear on pose (+23), color (+18), and approach direction (+18)--factors where goal-level instructions provide no guidance. Overall, fine-grained language should augment goal-level instructions: specifying how to execute alongside what to achieve. Project page: https://finevla.xlang.ai/

</details>


### [154] [Riding the Shifting Potential: When Reactive Control Suffices for Multi-Goal Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27314)
*Vito Mengers,Oliver Brock*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于零空间投影的图结构世界模型，通过动态优先级解决多目标冲突，在非凸障碍导航和非凸物体推挤任务中表现优异，且无需重训练即可迁移到真实机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为反应式控制由于目标冲突导致局部最小值而不足以解决多目标任务，作者认为这一限制源于静态编码无法反映目标间的交互动态，因此需要一种能动态处理目标优先级的方法。

Method: 在图结构世界模型中引入零空间投影：根据当前状态连续确定目标优先级，将低优先级梯度投影到高优先级梯度的零空间中，从而在冲突产生处就地解决冲突。

Result: 在非凸障碍导航和非凸物体推挤任务中，该方法在100个配置上达到100%成功率，而最速下降基线为0%，扩散策略约55%；同一机制可直接迁移到真实机器人，处理感知和运动学约束。

Conclusion: 反应式控制足以胜任多目标任务，关键在于动态编码目标交互结构而非静态处理，零空间投影提供了一种优雅的冲突解决机制。

Abstract: Reactive control is often considered insufficient for multi-objective tasks because conflicting objectives give rise to local minima. We argue this limitation is not inherent but arises from static encodings that fail to reflect how objectives currently interact. We exploit the interaction structure encoded in a graph-based world model by extending it with nullspace projections: conflicts are resolved where they arise by projecting lower-priority gradients into the nullspace of higher-priority ones, with priorities determined continuously from the current state. We demonstrate this in two domains where conflicts between objectives are central: navigation around non-convex obstacles, where static potential fields fundamentally fail, and planar pushing of non-convex objects, where our method achieves $100\%$ success across one-hundred configurations versus $0\%$ for the steepest-descent baseline and ${\sim}55\%$ for diffusion policy, without demonstrations or retraining. The same formulation transfers directly to a real robot with additional perceptual and kinematic constraints, accommodating them through the same mechanism.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [155] [BrickAnything: Geometry-Conditioned Buildable Brick Generation with Structure-Aware Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26182)
*Zhengyang Ni,Feng Yan,Yu Guo,Fei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出BrickAnything，一种基于几何条件的自回归框架，能从3D表示生成可建造的砖结构，通过结构感知树分词、偏好对齐训练和有效性约束解码，实现高几何保真度和物理可实现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有砖结构生成方法存在两个问题：启发式优化在目标形状不可行时失效；序列生成方法未显式建模3D几何和装配关系，导致生成结果不稳定。

Method: 1) 以点云作为统一几何接口；2) 提出结构感知树分词（structure-aware tree tokenization），基于局部附着关系表示砖结构；3) 偏好对齐后训练（preference-based alignment post-training）；4) 有效性约束解码（validity-constrained decoding）与自适应回滚（adaptive rollback）。

Result: BrickAnything能生成几何忠实且物理可实现的砖结构；所提分词方法相比传统排序策略有效减少回滚和重新生成。

Conclusion: 提出了一个完整的自回归生成框架，通过结构化的序列建模和解码策略，解决了砖结构生成中的几何保真和结构稳定性问题。

Abstract: Generating physically buildable brick structures from 3D shapes requires more than geometric reconstruction: the output must also satisfy discrete part constraints and structural stability. Existing brick generation methods either rely on heuristic optimization, which can break down when the target 3D shape does not admit a feasible structure under predefined constraints, or generate brick sequences without explicitly modeling the underlying 3D geometry and assembly relations. In this work, we present BrickAnything, a geometry-conditioned autoregressive framework for generating buildable brick structures from diverse 3D representations. BrickAnything uses point clouds as a unified geometric interface and predicts brick sequences that reconstruct the target shape under assembly constraints. To model structural dependencies among bricks, we introduce a structure-aware tree tokenization, which represents brick structures through local attachment relations. This formulation makes sequence generation more consistent with the physical construction process, and reduces invalid intermediate states. We further introduce preference-based alignment post-training, validity-constrained decoding and adaptive rollback to improve buildability objectives such as stability and geometric fidelity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BrickAnything produces geometrically faithful and physically realizable brick structures, and that the proposed tokenization effectively reduces rollback and regeneration compared with conventional ordering strategies.

</details>


### [156] [Can LLMs Introspect? A Reality Check](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26242)
*Shashwat Singh,Tal Linzen,Shauli Ravfogel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文质疑了大型语言模型能否真正内省其内部状态的主张。借鉴人类元认知研究，作者指出当前研究混淆了真正的内省与基于表面线索的模式匹配，并提出两个实验重新评估后发现：模型无法区分内部状态篡改与输入操纵，且仅依赖输入的分类器能达到与模型自身内省相当的性能。结论是现有证据不足以证明LLM具备元认知监控能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究声称LLM能够检测和报告自身内部状态，但作者认为这些结论可能为时过早。受人类元认知研究启发，需要区分真正的内省与基于表面线索的模式匹配，且仅靠行为证据不足以建立强有力的内省主张。

Method: 作者重新审视了两个评估范式：(1) 检测模型内部状态是否被篡改——对比模型区分内部状态干预与输入操纵的能力；(2) 模型预测源自自身隐藏状态的标签——引入仅依赖输入的分类器进行对比，并设计了重新标注的控制设置，使模型无法依赖任务语义而必须依靠内部表征。

Result: (1) 模型无法可靠区分内部状态干预与输入操纵，说明其成功仅反映检测异常的能力，而非对内省状态的识别；(2) 仅依赖输入的分类器实现了与模型自身内省预测相当的性能；在重新标注的控制设置中，模型表现接近随机水平。

Conclusion: 当前证据不足以证明LLM具备元认知监控能力。所谓的"内省"表现可能源于对表面线索的模式匹配或对任务语义的利用，而非真正的自我意识访问。研究呼吁对LLM内部状态的声称持更严谨的态度。

Abstract: Can large language models detect and report their own internal states? A number of studies have argued that the answer to this question is yes. We argue, based on lessons from human metacognition research, that this conclusion may be premature: to be convinced of this conclusion we need to distinguish genuine introspection from pattern matching based on surface-level cues. Furthermore, we argue that behavioral evidence alone is inherently insufficient to establish strong introspective claims.
  We re-examine two recently introduced evaluation paradigms in light of this consideration. In the first paradigm, models are expected to detect whether their internal states have been tampered with. We find that models cannot reliably distinguish such interventions on their internal states from manipulations of the input, suggesting that their success in the original studies reflects their ability to detect anomalies more generally, as opposed to interventions on their internal states in particular. In the second paradigm we examine, models are tasked with predicting labels derived from their own hidden states. Here, we find that classifiers that only have access to the input achieve equivalent performance to the model's own in-context predictions, indicating that the original results do not conclusively demonstrate that the model has privileged access to its internal representations. We further introduce a relabeled control setting, where models cannot rely on the semantics of the task to solve it, and instead must rely on the internal representation; models perform closer to chance on this better-controlled version of the task. Taken together, these results indicate that current evidence is insufficient to establish that LLMs display metacognitive monitoring.

</details>


### [157] [Is Agent Memory a Database? Rethinking Data Foundations for Long-Term AI Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26252)
*Abdelghny Orogat,Essam Mansour*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将长期AI代理内存视为一种新的数据管理工作负载，通过GEM（受控演化内存）形式化，用四个状态级操作符替代记录级数据库操作，并论证记录级系统无法满足内存正确性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI代理内存系统和数据库范式将内存视为存储，将正确性定位于记录、嵌入或边层面，仅满足长期内存的部分需求，导致四种常见故障模式：无节制增长、缺失语义修订、容量驱动遗忘、只读检索。

Method: 提出GEM形式化框架，用四个状态级操作符（摄取、修订、遗忘、检索）替代记录级数据库操作，定义六条正确性条件来约束状态演化，并通过三个结构性观察论证记录级系统无法满足这些条件。在属性图后端实现MemState原型验证可行性。

Result: MemState原型验证了GEM抽象在属性图后端上的可行性，同时揭示了与原生引擎之间的差距。

Conclusion: 长期AI代理内存需要一种新的以内存为中心的数据管理工作负载范式，而非将内存视为存储。作者提出三个研究方向来定义这一新领域。

Abstract: Long-running AI agents need persistent memory. Memory supports learning across sessions, reduces repeated context injection, and enables auditing of past decisions. Current agent memory systems and database paradigms treat memory as storage. They localize correctness at records, embeddings, or edges. Each supplies only some of the capabilities that long-term memory requires. The result is four recurring failure modes: unregulated growth, missing semantic revision, capacity-driven forgetting, and read-only retrieval. In our vision, long-term agent memory is a new data-management workload. Its correctness is a property of the state trajectory, not of individual records. We formalize this as Governed Evolving Memory (GEM). GEM replaces record-level database operations with four state-level operators: ingestion, revision, forgetting, and retrieval. Six correctness conditions govern how the state evolves. Three structural observations establish that no record-level system can satisfy these conditions, regardless of the storage model. We realize the abstraction in MemState, a prototype on a property-graph backend. MemState validates feasibility and exposes the gap to a native engine. We outline three research directions that define memory-centric data management as a workload.

</details>


### [158] [Personalizing Embodied Multimodal Large Language Model Agents over Long-term User Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26256)
*Jeongeun Lee,Chanyoung Park,Dongha Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: POLAR是一个基于多模态记忆增强的框架，通过构建包含语义记忆和情景记忆的多模态知识图谱，使具身智能体能够利用长期用户交互历史实现个性化任务执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实场景中，个性化辅助需要智能体从长期交互中隐式推断用户意图，而非仅遵循通用指令或识别物体类别，现有MLLM具身智能体缺乏对长期个性化上下文的利用能力。

Method: 提出POLAR框架，将历史交互组织为多模态知识图谱，包含语义记忆（个性化上下文和视觉概念）和情景记忆（智能体轨迹等经验），通过检索相关记忆来解读当前请求并指导任务执行。

Result: 在多种MLLM骨干网络和评估场景下，POLAR的记忆机制持续提升任务性能，尤其在需要跨交互推理、多跳推理或追踪用户上下文动态变化时效果显著。

Conclusion: 记忆机制能有效利用长期交互累积的信息，是提升个性化具身智能体在复杂场景中表现的关键。

Abstract: Multimodal large language model (MLLM)-based embodied agents have shown strong potential for solving complex tasks in physical environments. However, personalized assistance requires more than following generic instruction or recognizing object categories. In real-world scenarios, the intended target is often specified only implicitly through prior interactions, requiring agents to leverage personalized context accumulated over time. In this work, we propose POLAR, a multiomodal memory-augmented framework for personalized embodied agents over long-term user interactions. POLAR organizes prior interactions into a multimodal knowledge graph that captures semantic memory for personalized context and visual concepts, and episodic memory for embodied experiences such as agent trajectories. To execute embodied tasks, POLAR retrieves relevant memories to interpret the current request and guide task execution. We evaluate POLAR across multiple MLLM backbones and diverse evaluation scenarios to study the role of memory in long-term personalization. Results show that the proposed memory mechanism consistently improves performance by enabling more effective use of information accumulated over prior interactions. The gains are especially pronounced when the agents are required to reason across multiple interactions, perform multi-hop inference, or tracking updates in user-specific context over time.

</details>


### [159] [Constraint acquisition needs better benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26279)
*Rafał Stachowiak,Tomasz P. Pawlak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MPMMine基准套件，旨在系统评估利用领域知识工件发现、验证和增强数学规划模型的算法，解决了现有基准在约束获取研究中缺乏领域知识工件、结构不统一、可重复性差等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前约束获取及数学规划模型验证与增强的研究缺乏合适的基准，现有基准面向求解器评估而非CA算法，缺乏领域知识工件、结构松散、问题处理不一致，阻碍了方法的可重复性和跨研究对比。

Method: 提出MPMMine基准套件，遵循一致性、标准化、完整性、可扩展性、开放性和版本控制原则，采用MiniZinc、CommonMark、JSON等开放格式，为每个问题提供多个模型、数十个实例及数千个解与非解，并包含自然语言描述以支持文本到模型方法。

Result: 提供了一个结构统一、覆盖整数与连续域、包含多种领域知识工件（模型、实例、解、非解、自然语言描述）的标准化基准套件。

Conclusion: MPMMine填补了CA与MP模型验证增强研究的基准空白，有望提升该领域研究的可重复性、可比性和方法成熟度。

Abstract: Constraint Acquisition (CA) and related research on the validation and enhancement of Mathematical Programming (MP) models from domain knowledge artifacts are currently limited by inadequate benchmarks. This deficiency impedes reproducibility and cross-study comparability, slowing the maturation of CA methods. Existing benchmarks were designed for solver evaluation rather than for assessing CA algorithms. They are loosely organized, treat individual problems inconsistently, and omit the domain knowledge artifacts required by CA methods. This work presents MPMMine, a benchmark suite designed to assess algorithms that discover, validate, and enhance MP models using diverse domain knowledge artifacts. MPMMine is guided by consistency, standardization, completeness, extensibility, openness, and version control. It adopts a uniform structure and relies on open formats: MiniZinc, CommonMark, and JSON. It provides multiple models per problem, tens of instances per model, and thousands of solutions and non-solutions in both integer and continuous domains, alongside natural-language descriptions to support text-to-model methods.

</details>


### [160] [Your Agents Are Aging Too: Agent Lifespan Engineering for Deployed Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26302)
*Jianing Zhu,Yeonju Ro,John Robertson,Kevin Wang,Junbo Li,Haris Vikalo,Aditya Akella,Zhangyang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AgingBench，一个纵向可靠性基准，用于评估AI智能体在部署后随时间退化的四种机制（压缩、干扰、修订、维护），并通过时间依赖图和反事实探针诊断记忆管道中的故障阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅在部署首日评估智能体，忽略了其作为持久系统随时间退化的关键问题，无法回答"智能体在部署后能保持多久的可靠性"这一系统工程问题。

Method: ①将智能体退化分为四种机制：压缩老化、干扰老化、修订老化、维护老化；②使用时间依赖图与配对反事实探针，对记忆管道的写入、检索、利用三个阶段进行诊断分析。

Result: 跨7个场景、14个模型、约400次运行（8-200会话）的实验表明：行为测试可能正常但事实精度已经下降；派生状态追踪可能在单一模型内急剧崩溃；相同错误答案可能因诊断结果不同需要不同的修复策略。

Conclusion: 可靠的智能体部署需要纵向寿命评估、机制级诊断和分阶段针对性修复，而非仅仅追求更强的首日模型。

Abstract: Long-lived AI agents are increasingly deployed as persistent operational systems, yet they are still evaluated like freshly initialized models. Day-one benchmarks miss a basic systems question: how long does an agent remain reliable after deployment? Even when model weights are frozen, an agent's effective state keeps changing as it compresses interaction history, retrieves from a growing memory store, revises facts after updates, and undergoes routine maintenance. Reliability therefore becomes a lifespan property of the full agent harness, not only a snapshot property of the base model. We introduce AgingBench, a longitudinal reliability benchmark for agent lifespan engineering: measuring not only whether deployed agents degrade, but what form the degradation takes and where repair should target. AgingBench organizes agent aging into four mechanisms: compression aging, interference aging, revision aging, and maintenance aging. To diagnose these failures, AgingBench uses temporal dependency graphs and paired counterfactual probes that produce diagnostic profiles for the write, retrieval, and utilization stages of the memory pipeline. Across 7 scenarios, 14 models, multiple memory policies, and both runner-controlled and autonomous agents, over ~400 runs spanning 8 - 200 sessions show that agent aging is not one-dimensional: behavioral tests can remain clean while factual precision decays; derived-state tracking can collapse sharply within a single model; and the same wrong answer can require different repairs depending on what the diagnostic profile points to. These results suggest that reliable agent deployment requires lifespan evaluation, mechanism-level diagnosis, and stage-targeted repair, not only stronger day-one models.

</details>


### [161] [Experiments in Agentic AI for Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26305)
*Judy Fox,Geoffrey Fox*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了两个自主AI智能体框架：DeepTS/DeepCollector用于自动化时间序列数据集的大规模整理，以及DeepScribe用于将物理讲座转化为结构化科学报告。两者采用本地身体+远程大脑的混合架构，通过系统工程技术克服当前大模型的上下文和推理限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前最先进的大语言模型存在上下文长度和推理能力限制，难以直接支持复杂科学工作流。作者希望构建自主AI智能体来自动化科学数据整理和报告生成，突破现有模型的能力瓶颈。

Method: 采用混合架构：本地Python编排器（Local Body）调用云端大语言模型后端（Remote Brain），运行在Google Colab上。具体包含：1）DeepTS/DeepCollector：自动化时序数据集整理、提取和去重；2）DeepScribe：将密集的物理讲座视频自动转化为结构化科学报告。系统工程技术包括粒度属性提取（Cellular RAG）、远程数据检查和分布式并发控制。

Result: 成功展示了自主AI智能体如何克服当前大模型的上下文和推理限制，支持科学工作流。两个系统分别实现了时序数据的自动化整理和物理讲座的结构化报告生成。

Conclusion: 提出了DeepTS向深度知识图谱的泛化方案，并讨论了该概念方法在高能物理（DeepQCD）中的应用前景。

Abstract: This paper details two novel frameworks for developing autonomous, agentic AI in scientific workflows. Both systems leverage a hybrid Local Body, Remote Brain architecture via Google Colab, utilizing Python-based local orchestrators to invoke large language model (LLM) cloud backends. The first agent, DeepTS/DeepCollector, automates the large-scale curation, extraction, and deduplication of time-series datasets. The second, DeepScribe, is an autonomous presentation analyzer that converts visually dense, mathematically complex physics lectures into structured scientific reports. Through practical systems engineering-such as granular attribute extraction (Cellular RAG), remote data inspection, and distributed concurrency controls-we demonstrate how agentic AI can overcome the context and reasoning limitations of current state-of-the-art systems to rigorously support scientific workflows. Finally, we outline a generalization of DeepTS to support deep knowledge graphs and discuss the application of this conceptual approach to high-energy physics (DeepQCD).

</details>


### [162] [Anchor: Mitigating Artifact Drift in Agent Benchmark Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26321)
*Maksim Ivanov,Abhijay Rana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Anchor，一种将领域专家的工作流规范形式化为约束优化程序的任务生成管道，可联合生成自然语言指令、环境配置、求解器验证的正确答案和状态验证器，解决了企业环境创建中的"artifact drift"问题。基于此构建了包含300个长周期任务的ERP-Bench基准测试，发现前沿模型在26.1%的试验中满足显式约束，但仅17.4%达到完全最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业AI agent的训练和评估环境在真实性、可验证性和规模之间难以平衡，现有环境创建存在"artifact drift"问题，即指令、环境、生成器和验证器之间缺乏一致性，导致任务不可解、可被奖励黑客攻击或不一致。

Method: 提出Anchor任务生成管道：将领域专家的业务流程规范形式化为约束优化程序，从单一参数化规范中联合生成自然语言指令、环境配置、求解器验证的正确答案和基于状态的验证器。改变参数即可生成不同难度和已知最优解的新任务。

Result: 构建了ERP-Bench基准（300个长周期任务），发现生成参数可预测实际难度；前沿模型在26.1%的试验中满足显式约束，但仅17.4%达到完全最优解。

Conclusion: Anchor和ERP-Bench为构建可审计的经济价值型agent评估环境提供了具体方案，已开源任务生成器和数据集。

Abstract: AI agents are beginning to complete valuable, long-horizon business operations tasks, but training and evaluation environments for enterprise work still struggle to balance realism, verifiability, and scale. Environment and task creation frequently suffers from a failure mode we call artifact drift: when instructions, environments, oracles, and verifiers are created by loosely coupled processes, they frequently disagree on what a task requires, producing environments that are unsolvable, reward-hackable, or inconsistent. We introduce Anchor, a task-generation pipeline that formalizes domain experts' specifications of business workflows into constraint optimization programs. From a single parametric specification, the pipeline jointly produces a natural-language instruction, environment configuration, solver-certified ground-truth solution, and state-based verifier. With Anchor, altering parameters yields new tasks with controlled difficulty and known optimal solutions, producing harness-agnostic environments whose rewards depend solely on end-state business correctness. We apply Anchor to produce ERP-Bench: a benchmark of 300 long-horizon tasks spanning procurement and manufacturing workflows in a production-grade ERP system. We find that generation parameters predict realized difficulty, and that frontier models satisfy explicit task constraints in 26.1% of trials but reach a fully optimal solution in only 17.4% of trials. Overall, we show that Anchor and ERP-Bench offer a concrete recipe for building auditable evaluation environments for economically valuable agent work. We release the task generator and ERP-Bench dataset at erpbench.ai

</details>


### [163] [OmniToM: Benchmarking Theory of Mind in LLMs via Explicit Belief Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26322)
*Adam Bawatneh,Sagar Sapkota,Amrit Singh Bedi,Santu Karmaker,Mubarak Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OmniToM是一个评估大语言模型心智理论（ToM）能力的新基准，通过要求模型显式提取和标注故事中所有角色的信念结构（包括22,343个标注的信念命题），而非仅通过最终问答判断，揭示了当前LLMs在将叙述事实转化为角色信念和共享心智状态时存在知识获取和表征决策瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM的ToM评估仅依赖最终问答，无法判断模型是否真正构建了底层的心理状态表征，特别是在涉及分歧、演变或错误信念的场景中。

Method: 基于895个ToMBench故事构建，通过LLM辅助标注流水线生成22,343个信念命题；采用两阶段评估：信念提取（抽取社会动态相关信念）和信念标注（七维模式标注，包括递归顺序、真值状态、知识获取等）。

Result: 零样本评估显示，当前LLMs在将叙述事实转化为角色信念和共享心智状态时存在知识获取和表征决策方面的瓶颈。

Conclusion: OmniToM揭示了现有LLM在actor-specific信念追踪上的根本性缺陷，为ToM能力评估提供了更精细、结构化的基准框架。

Abstract: Theory of Mind (ToM), the ability to infer others' knowledge, intentions, and emotions, is commonly evaluated in large language models (LLMs) using end-point question answering, where performance is judged solely by the final answer to a social reasoning query. This paradigm obscures whether the model actually constructs the underlying mental-state representations required for robust reasoning, particularly in scenarios involving divergent, evolving, or mistaken beliefs. In order to address this research gap, we introduce OmniToM, a benchmark that directly evaluates these representations by requiring explicit modeling of belief structures for all relevant actors within a narrative. These structures are composed of belief propositions: minimal statements of what an actor takes to be true about the world or another actor's mental state, allowing knowledge, intentions, emotions, and false beliefs to be analyzed in a common format. Models are evaluated in two stages: Stage 1: Belief Extraction, which extracts from the story the beliefs relevant to its social dynamics, and Stage 2: Belief Labeling, which assigns each belief a seven-dimensional schema label covering recursive order, truth status, knowledge access, explicitness, content type, mental source, and context. Built from 895 stories from the existing ToMBench story corpus and augmented with 22,343 labeled belief propositions, OmniToM uses a human-calibrated LLM-assisted annotation pipeline. Across diverse models in zero-shot evaluation, OmniToM reveals an actor-specific belief-tracking bottleneck: current LLMs struggle with the knowledge-access and representational decisions required to transform narrative facts into actors' beliefs and shared mental states.

</details>


### [164] [JobBench: Aligning Agent Work With Human Will](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26329)
*Yuetai Li,Yichen Feng,Zhangchen Xu,Zixian Ma,Kaiyuan Zheng,Fengqing Jiang,Xinghua Sun,Rulin Shao,Zichen Chen,Yue Huang,Xinyang Han,Brian Lee,Kayla Xu,Shenglai Zeng,Hang Hua,Xiangliang Zhang,Basel Alomair,Ranjay Krishna,Luke Zettlemoyer,Pang Wei Koh,Bhaskar Ramasubramanian,Luyao Niu,Xiang Yue,Radha Poovendran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了JobBench基准，基于专家认定的高优先级可委派工作流程评估AI代理，而非仅关注经济价值。涵盖35个职业的130个代理任务，最强模型Claude Opus在Claude Code下仅达45.9%的准确率，旨在推动AI从替代人类转向赋能人类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有职业AI代理基准主要基于经济价值评估，讲述的是"替代"故事，忽视了人类真正需要的委派需求。作者希望将目标从"替代"转向"增强"，构建人类真正想要委派的AI代理，而非仅追求经济价值最大化。

Method: JobBench覆盖35个职业的130个代理任务，每个任务包含异构参考文件的工作区，要求代理在杂乱信息流中推理。输出通过基于事实的评分链评估，平均每任务35.6个二分类标准。评估了36个模型。

Result: 最强模型Claude Opus在Claude Code下仅达到45.9%的准确率，表明当前AI代理在真实专业工作场景下表现有限。

Conclusion: 希望JobBench能够引导社区从"替代"转向"增强"的劳动力市场效应，构建执行人类真正想要委派任务的AI代理。

Abstract: Current benchmarks for occupational AI agents are scoped primarily by economic values, telling a replacement story. We introduce JobBench, which evaluates AI agents on the workflows that experts identify as high-priority for delegation, empowering humans based on their needs instead of replacing them with GDP value. JobBench covers 130 agentic tasks across 35 occupations. Each task is packaged as a workspace of heterogeneous reference files, requiring the agent to reason through the cluttered information streams of real professional work. Outputs are graded by a fact-anchored chain of rubrics, averaging 35.6 binary criteria per task. We evaluate 36 models; the strongest, Claude Opus~4.7 under Claude Code, reaches only 45.9 %. We hope JobBench shifts the community's target labour-market effect from replacement to enhancement: building agents that do what humans actually want delegated, not only what is most economically valuable.

</details>


### [165] [Managing Uncertainty in LLM-Generated Procedural Knowledge for Virtual Laboratory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26333)
*Polychronis Karpodinis,Dimitris Kalles*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个原型框架，利用结构化领域表示和LLM生成的状态转移样本，提取候选程序规则并转化为显式约束，以修复不确定的程序步骤，从而管理LLM生成程序知识中的不确定性，应用于虚拟实验室规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教育型虚拟实验室可扩展实验训练，但编写新的模拟实验程序成本高昂。LLM可辅助生成实验程序，但其输出存在遗漏步骤、顺序错误、逻辑不正确等问题，不能直接执行。因此需要管理LLM生成程序知识中的不确定性。

Method: 提出一个原型框架，使用结构化领域表示和不确定的LLM生成状态-转移样本，提取候选程序规则，将其转化为显式且可检查的约束，并用这些约束来修复不确定的程序步骤。

Result: 在涉及实验仪器、容器、工具和材料转移操作的虚拟实验室领域进行了方法展示。

Conclusion: 该框架可减少程序不确定性，且其底层问题（管理结构化交互环境中行动规划的不确定程序知识）具有更广泛的通用性。

Abstract: Educational virtual laboratories can make experimental training more scala-ble, adaptive, and accessible, especially when students have limited access to physical laboratory facilities. However, authoring new simulated laboratory procedures remains costly: educators must describe new equipment, define how instruments and materials interact, and specify valid procedural flows that can be executed or assessed inside the virtual environment. Large lan-guage models can assist in this authoring process by generating detailed ex-perimental procedures, but their output should not be treated as directly exe-cutable plans. They may omit necessary actions, arrange steps in the wrong order, or produce instructions that are logically incorrect or incompatible with the laboratory equipment. This paper presents a prototype framework for managing uncertainty in LLM-generated procedural knowledge for virtu-al laboratory planning. The framework aims to reduce procedural uncertainty by using structured domain representations and uncertain LLM-generated state-transition samples to extract candidate procedural rules, transform them into explicit and inspectable constraints, and use them to repair uncertain procedural steps. Although the motivating domain refers to educational vir-tual laboratories, the underlying problem is more general: managing uncer-tain procedural knowledge for action planning in structured interactive envi-ronments. We illustrate the approach in a virtual laboratory domain involving laboratory instruments, containers, tools, and material-transfer actions.

</details>


### [166] [ScientistOne: Towards Human-Level Autonomous Research via Chain-of-Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26340)
*Rui Meng,Bhavana Dalvi Mishra,Jiefeng Chen,Chun-Liang Li,Palash Goyal,Mihir Parmar,Yiwen Song,Yale Song,Rajarishi Sinha,Parthasarathy Ranganathan,Burak Gokturk,Jinsung Yoon,Tomas Pfister*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Chain-of-Evidence (CoE)可验证性框架、ScientistOne端到端自主研究系统及CoE Audit事后审计方法，解决自主研究代理输出中的虚假引用、不可复现分数等问题。ScientistOne在75篇论文、5个前沿研究任务中实现零虚假引用、完美分数验证，并在5个任务上达到或超越人类专家水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主研究代理虽能产出高水平论文，但其输出存在表面评估无法检测的可验证性问题（虚假引用、不可复现的分数、方法与实现不符），亟需系统性的验证方案。

Method: 提出三部分贡献：1) CoE框架，要求每个声明可追溯至证据源；2) ScientistOne系统，在文献综述、方案发现和论文撰写过程中天然维护证据链；3) CoE Audit事后审计，包含分数验证、规范违规、引用验证和方法-代码对齐四项完整性检查。

Result: 在75篇论文、5个系统、5个前沿研究任务中，所有基线系统都存在系统性故障模式：虚假引用率达21%，分数验证通过率低至42%，方法-代码对齐度为20%-80%。ScientistOne实现零虚假引用（0/337）、完美分数验证（12/12）、最高方法-代码对齐度（14/15），在全部5个任务上达到或超越人类专家水平。

Conclusion: ScientistOne在医学影像、细粒度识别、3D感知和语言建模等6个额外任务上展现出泛化能力，在Parameter Golf上达到SOTA，在MLE-Bench任务上获得金牌（基线系统完全失败），证明基于证据链的自主研究系统具有更高的可信度和实用性。

Abstract: Autonomous research agents produce competitive solutions and professional-looking manuscripts, yet their outputs contain verifiability failures undetectable by surface-level evaluation: fabricated citations, unreproducible scores, and method descriptions that diverge from the implementation. We address this through three contributions. First, Chain-of-Evidence (CoE), a verifiability framework requiring every claim to be traceable to its evidence source. Second, ScientistOne, an end-to-end autonomous research system that maintains evidence chains by construction throughout literature review, solution discovery, and paper writing. Third, CoE Audit, a post-hoc audit whose four integrity checks -- score verification, specification violation, reference verification, and method-code alignment -- apply uniformly to all systems. Across 75 papers spanning five systems and five frontier research tasks, every baseline exhibits at least one systematic failure mode: hallucinated reference rates reach 21%, score verification passes in as few as 42% of papers, and method-code alignment ranges from 20% to 80%. ScientistOne achieves zero hallucinated references (0/337), perfect score verification (12/12), and the highest method-code alignment (14/15), while matching or exceeding human expert performance on all five tasks. ScientistOne further generalizes to six additional tasks spanning medical imaging, fine-grained recognition, 3D perception, and language modeling, achieving state-of-the-art on Parameter Golf and gold medals on MLE-Bench tasks where baselines fail entirely.

</details>


### [167] [Automatic Layer Selection for Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26366)
*Xinpeng Wang,William Cao,Andrew Gordon Wilson,Zhe Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的自动层选择方法FEPoID，用于从大语言模型中间层检测幻觉，并引入截断策略增强幻觉信号，在问答和摘要检测任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽发现幻觉信号在大模型中间层比最后一层更显著，但如何自动选择高性能层仍未被充分探索，缺乏系统化的原则性方法。

Method: 首先提出多个关于中间层产生幻觉信号的假设并评估相应的自动选层标准，发现均不理想；进而提出新标准FEPoID（有效本征维度的第一个峰值），用于自动识别最优或接近最优的中间层；同时引入一种简单有效的截断策略来放大幻觉相关信号。

Result: FEPoID在多种LLM架构、规模、任务（问答和摘要幻觉检测）上一致地识别出最优或接近最优的层，超越现有选层标准和幻觉检测基线方法，且无需训练、计算开销极小。

Conclusion: 验证了自动选择中间层对幻觉检测的有效性，FEPoID提供了一种轻量级、通用且高效的解决方案，截断策略进一步提升了检测性能。

Abstract: Recent studies on hallucination detection have shown that hallucination-related signals are more strongly encoded in intermediate layers than in the final layer of large language models (LLMs). Although a growing body of work has sought to exploit this property for hallucination detection, how to automate the selection of high-performing layers remains underexplored, and principled methods for this purpose are still lacking. To address this gap, we first propose several hypotheses for why such signals emerge in intermediate layers and evaluate corresponding criteria for automatic layer selection across diverse LLM architectures, scales, and tasks, covering both question answering and summarization hallucination detection benchmarks. However, we find that none of these criteria consistently delivers satisfactory performance. We therefore propose a new selection criterion, First Effective Peak of Intrinsic Dimension (FEPoID), which consistently identify optimal or near-optimal layers and outperforms both the aforementioned criteria and existing hallucination detection baselines. FEPoID is training-free and incurs negligible computational overhead. In addition, we study the generation behaviors of LLMs and introduce a simple yet effective truncation strategy, which further amplifies hallucination-related signals and substantially improves overall detection performance. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/DesoloYw/Automatic-Layer-Selection-for-Hallucination-Detection.git

</details>


### [168] [Exploiting Local Dynamics Regularity for Reusable Skills in Offline Hierarchical RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26371)
*Sarthak Dayal,Abhinav Peri,Carl Qi,Claas Voelcker,Alexander Levine,Caleb Chuck,Amy Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CARL通过对比学习对齐局部动态与动作序列，学习可复用的技能表示，并在复杂人形环境和OGBench基准中提升了分层强化学习的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分层强化学习（HRL）方法难以学到真正可复用的技能，导致长程任务效率不高。

Method: 提出CARL算法，利用对比学习对齐局部动态（不同全局上下文下的局部转移）与所需的动作序列，从而学习何时重用何种技能。

Result: 在复杂人形环境中实现了有意义的技能聚类，在与HIQL集成后提升了OGBench基准的下游任务表现。

Conclusion: 通过对齐局部上下文与动作序列，CARL有效学习到可复用技能，为HRL提供了一种通用且有效的表示学习机制。

Abstract: Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning (HRL) promises to solve long-horizon Reinforcement Learning (RL) tasks more efficiently than non-hierarchical counterparts by discovering and reusing temporally-extended skills. However, obtaining skills that are actually reusable remains an open challenge. Towards this end, we focus on abstractions that exploit the intuition of local dynamics: local transitions in different global contexts require similar kinds of action sequences. By aligning these contexts with the action sequences they require, we are able to learn which skills to reuse and where to reuse them. In principle, this information should benefit many HRL algorithms, where high-level policies have to reason about the low-level skills they use. The resulting algorithm CARL (Contrastive Action-based Representations for Reusable Local Control) shows both qualitative clustering of meaningful skills in complex humanoid environments and improved downstream performance on the OGBench benchmark when integrated with HIQL.

</details>


### [169] [Advancing Creative Physical Intelligence in Large Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26396)
*Cheng Qian,Hyeonjeong Ha,Jiayu Liu,Jeonghwan Kim,Emre Can Acikgoz,Bingxuan Li,Kunlun Zhu,Jiateng Liu,Aditi Tiwari,Zhenhailong Wang,Xiusi Chen,Mahdi Namazifar,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MM-CreativityBench基准，用于评估多模态大模型在开放环境中基于物能性的创造性工具使用能力。实验发现当前LMMs因缺乏接地探索而表现不佳，为此提出基于物能性接地对齐的偏好学习方法（DPO），结合物能性知识库监督，显著提升了实体选择准确率并减少了幻觉和接地错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LMMs在感知和推理方面进步迅速，但能否在开放环境中进行超越模式识别的视觉接地创造性问题求解尚不明确。创造性问题求解需要识别场景中元素的非显而易见的物理可行重用途，这一能力在现有基准中未得到充分测试。

Method: 提出MM-CreativityBench基准，包含富视觉、物理约束场景中的物能性接地创造性工具使用任务。针对LMMs接地探索不足的问题，提出物能性接地对齐方法：将创造性工具使用建模为偏好学习问题，使用直接偏好优化（DPO）鼓励模型偏好基于视觉证据的属性-物能性推理；同时引入物能性知识库监督，指导更广泛的实体探索和多轮规划。

Result: 实验表明，当前LMMs在MM-CreativityBench上表现不佳，失败原因非生成能力不足，而是无法维持接地探索——常忽略相关实体、未充分检查关键部件或幻觉出图像中不存在的属性。所提出的物能性接地对齐方法在正确实体和部件选择上取得一致性提升，同时大幅减少了幻觉和接地相关错误。

Conclusion: LMMs在创造性视觉问题求解中的主要瓶颈是接地探索不足而非生成能力；物能性接地对齐作为一种偏好学习方法，能有效缓解这一问题。

Abstract: Large multimodal models (LMMs) have rapidly advanced in perception and reasoning; however, it remains unclear whether these capabilities generalize to discovering visually grounded solutions in open-ended environments, beyond pattern recognition. In such settings, intelligence requires more than answering well-posed questions: it involves identifying how elements in a scene can be repurposed in non-obvious yet physically feasible ways. This form of creative problem-solving is central to human intelligence, but remains largely untested in current benchmarks. To evaluate this ability, we introduce MM-CreativityBench, a benchmark for affordance-grounded creative tool use in visually rich, physically constrained environments. Each instance presents a scenario image with structured views of candidate entities and their parts, enabling fine-grained, interactive evaluation of how models iteratively inspect the scene, identify relevant affordances, and compose visually and physically grounded solutions. Our experiments show that current LMMs often fall short, not due to lack of generative capability, but because they do not sustain grounded exploration. Models often overlook relevant entities, under-examine critical parts, or hallucinate attributes not grounded in the image. Motivated by this failure mode, we propose affordance-grounded alignment, which casts creative tool use as a preference learning problem. Using Direct Preference Optimization, we encourage models to prefer attribute-affordance reasoning grounded in visual evidence over hallucinated alternatives. In addition, we incorporate supervision derived from an affordance knowledge base to guide broader entity exploration and multi-turn planning. Our results show consistent gains in selecting the correct entities and parts, while substantially reducing hallucination and grounding-related errors.

</details>


### [170] [From Static Context to Calibrated Interactive RL: Mitigating Distribution Shift in Multi-turn Dialogue with Aligned Simulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26403)
*Xiaohua Wang,Jiakang Yuan,Zisu Huang,Muzhao Tian,Changze Lv,Kaitao Song,Tao Chen,Xiaoqing Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从理论上证明了静态上下文RL和基于提示的模拟器交互RL都存在上下文分布偏移问题，该问题会随对话轮次二次方累积，严重降低对话质量。作者提出校准交互式RL框架，通过将模拟器与人类行为对齐来缩小模拟与现实差距，实验表明该方法显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究社区长期目标是开发高交互性的LLM对话智能体，但现有方法（基于固定离线日志的静态上下文RL和基于提示模拟器的交互RL）存在根本性限制，即上下文分布偏移问题，导致训练与真实对话场景不匹配。

Method: 提出校准交互式RL（Calibrated Interactive RL）统一框架：（1）将交互式RL与模拟器对齐相结合；（2）通过将模拟器与人类交互模式对齐，减少模拟到现实的差距；（3）缓解复合分布偏移问题。

Result: 在多个对话任务上验证了理论分析：（1）交互式RL通过缓解策略分布偏移，显著优于静态上下文基线；（2）校准模拟器进一步缩小了模拟与现实差距，达到了最先进的下游性能。

Conclusion: 上下文分布偏移是对话智能体训练中的关键问题，包含策略诱导偏移和模拟器诱导偏移两种来源。校准交互式RL通过统一交互学习和模拟器对齐，有效解决了这一问题，为构建高质量对话智能体提供了新方向。

Abstract: A long-standing goal of the research community is to develop highly interactive LLM-based dialogue agents. Recent research focuses on optimizing policies based on fixed offline logs (Static Context RL) or using a prompt-based simulator (Interactive RL). In this work, we theoretically show that both paradigms are fundamentally limited by context distribution shift--a mismatch between dialogue histories observed during training and those encountered in real conversations. This shift compounds quadratically over turns and severely degrades dialogue quality. Specifically, we attribute this shift to two distinct sources: (i) policy-induced shift, arising from training on static histories rather than self-generated trajectories; and (ii) simulator-induced shift, stemming from discrepancies between simulated and real human behaviors. To address these challenges, we propose Calibrated Interactive RL, a unified framework that couples interactive RL with simulator alignment. By aligning the simulator with human interaction patterns, our approach reduces the sim-to-real gap and mitigates compounding distribution shifts. Experiments across multiple dialogue tasks confirm our theoretical analysis: (i) Interactive RL significantly outperforms the Static Context baseline by mitigating policy distribution shift; and (ii) calibrating simulators with our alignment method further bridges the sim-to-real gap, yielding state-of-the-art downstream performance.

</details>


### [171] [Reasoning, Code, or Both? How Large Language Models Handle Variations in Math Questions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26414)
*Matthew Kutakh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了三种方法（CoT、PAL、SBSC）在数学推理鲁棒性上的表现，发现代码执行方法（PAL和SBSC）并未提升鲁棒性，CoT反而是最稳健的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在数学推理基准上表现出色，但遇到问题中姓名、数字等简单修改时性能会下降。代码执行方法被提出作为解决方案，但其对推理鲁棒性的影响尚未被系统测试。

Method: 在GSM-Symbolic数据集的1000个问题上，评估三种方法：CoT（纯推理）、PAL（单次代码执行）、SBSC（迭代代码执行）。所有方法均在Claude Haiku 4.5上运行，对比原始问题与修改后问题的准确率变化。

Result: CoT准确率下降1.3个百分点，1.8%的问题被扰动破坏；PAL下降1.7个百分点，3.1%被破坏；SBSC介于两者之间。差异未达统计显著（p=.096），但方向一致。

Conclusion: 代码执行方法（无论单次还是迭代）并未提升小学级别数学问题的推理鲁棒性，CoT反而更为稳健。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) achieve impressive accuracy on mathematical reasoning benchmarks, yet their performance drops when problems are modified with simple changes like different names or numbers. Code execution methods, which let models generate and run Python code instead of reasoning in natural language, have been proposed as a solution, but their effect on reasoning robustness (the ability to maintain accuracy across problem variations) has not been systematically tested. This study evaluates three approaches on 1,000 problems from the GSM-Symbolic dataset: pure reasoning using chain-of-thought (CoT) prompting, single-shot code execution using Program-Aided Language models (PAL), and iterative code execution using Step-by-Step Coding (SBSC). All three were run on paired original and modified problems using Claude Haiku 4.5. CoT was the most robust method, with an accuracy drop of 1.3 percentage points and 1.8% of problems breaking under perturbation. PAL was the least robust at 1.7 percentage points and 3.1% broke, with SBSC falling in between. Although these differences were not statistically significant ($p = .096$), the directional trend was consistent across all measures, suggesting that code execution, whether single-shot or iterative, does not improve reasoning robustness on grade-school-level problem variations.

</details>


### [172] [The MiniMax-M2 Series: Mini Activations Unleashing Max Real-World Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26494)
*MiniMax,:,Aili Chen,Aonian Li,Baichuan Zhou,Bangwei Gong,Binyang Jiang,Boji Dan,Changqing Yu,Chao Wang,Cheng Ma,Cheng Zhong,Cheng Zhu,Chengjun Xiao,Chengyi Yang,Chengyu Du,Chenyang Zhang,Chi Zhang,Chuangyi Huang,Chunhao Zhang,Chunhui Du,Chunyu Zhao,Congchao Guo,Da Chen,Deming Ding,Dianjun Sun,Dongyu Zhang,Enhui Yang,Fei Yu,Guang Zheng,Guodong Zheng,Guohong Li,Haichao Zhu,Haigang Zhou,Haimo Zhang,Han Ding,Hao Zhang,Haohai Sun,Haolin Lyu,Haonan Lu,Haoyu Wang,Huajie Shi,Huiyang Li,Jiacheng Chen,Jian Zhang,Jiaqi Zhuang,Jiaren Cai,Jiaxin Pan,Jiayao Li,Jiayuan Song,Jichuan Zhang,Jie Wang,Jihao Gu,Jin Zhu,Jingwei Dong,Jingyang Li,Jingyu Zhang,Jingze Zhuang,Jinhao Tian,Jinli Liu,Jinyi Hu,Jun Tao,Jun Zhang,Junbin Ruan,Junhao Xu,Junjie Yan,Junteng Liu,Junxian He,Kang Xu,Ke Ji,Ke Yang,Kecheng Xiao,Keyu Duan,Keyu Li,Le Han,Letian Ruan,Li Yuan,Lianfei Yu,Liheng Feng,Lijie Mo,Lin Li,Lingye Bao,Lingyu Yang,Lingyuan Zhou,Loki,Lu Chen,Lunbin Ceng,Ming Li,Ming Zhong,Mingliang Tao,Mingyuan Chi,Mujie Lin,Nan Hu,Ningxin Chen,Peiyin Zhu,Peng Gao,Pengcheng Gao,Pengfei Li,Penglin Li,Pengyu Zhao,Qibin Ren,Qidi Xu,Qihan Ren,Qile Li,Qin Wang,Quanliang Chen,Qunhong Ceng,Rong Tian,Rui Dong,Ruitao Leng,Ruize Zhang,Shanqi Liu,Shaoyu Chen,Sheng Jia,Shun Yao,Shuoran Zhao,Shuqi Yu,Sichen Li,Sicheng Pan,Songquan Zhu,Tengfei Li,Tian Xie,Tiancheng Qin,Tianrun Liang,Wei Liu,Weiqi Xu,Weitao Li,Weixiang Chen,Weiyu Cheng,Weiyu Zhang,Wenhu Chen,Wenqian Zhao,Xiancai Chen,Xiangjun Song,Xiangyuan Wang,Xiao Luo,Xiao Su,Xiaobo Li,Xiaodong Han,Xiaojie Wu,Xihao Song,Xingyi Han,Xinyu Guan,Xuan Lu,Xun Zou,Xunhao Lai,Xutong Li,Yan Gong,Yang Wang,Yang Xu,Yangsen Wang,Ye Tang,Yicheng Chen,Yinran Qiu,Yiqi Shi,Yiting Guo,Yiwen Huang,Yixuan Wang,Yongyi Hu,Yu Gao,Yu Zhang,Yuanxiang Ying,Yuanzhen Zhang,Yubo Wang,Yuchen Song,Yufeng Yang,Yuhang Meng,Yuhang Miao,Yuhao Li,Yujie Liu,Yulin Hu,Yunan Huang,Yunji Li,Yunyi Huang,Yusen Zhang,Yusu Hong,Yutao Xie,Yutong Zhang,Yuwen Liao,Yuxuan Shi,Yuze Wenren,Zebin Li,Zehan Li,Zejian Luo,Zeyu Jin,Zeyuan Sun,Zhanpeng Zhou,Zhaochen Su,Zhendong Li,Zhengmao Zhu,Zhengyuan Peng,Zhenhua Fan,Zhi Zhang,Zhichao Xu,Zhiheng Lv,Zhikang Xu,Zhitao He,Zhiwei He,Zhongyuan Li,Zibo Gao,Zijia Wu,Zijian Song,Zijian Zhou,Zijun Sun,Zishan Huang,Ziying Chen,Ziyue Ge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MiniMax-M2系列是基于混合专家架构的大语言模型，虽仅激活98亿参数（总参数2299亿），但通过端到端智能体部署设计，在智能体编程、深度搜索、办公任务和推理基准上达到前沿水平。其M2.7版本已初步实现自我进化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是通过"小激活"实现"大智能"，即在保持模型总参数规模的同时，大幅减少每次推理激活的参数数量，从而在有限计算资源下达到前沿性能，并专门针对智能体部署场景进行端到端优化。

Method: 方法包含三大组件：1) 智能体驱动的数据流水线，生成大规模、可验证的智能体编程和协作轨迹；2) Forge可扩展RL系统，支持长序列智能体轨迹训练，含窗口FIFO调度、前缀树合并、推理优化及训练-推理-智能体解耦；3) M2.7版本实现初步自演化，可自主调试训练和修改脚手架。

Result: M2系列以极少的激活参数（98亿/2299亿）在智能体编程、深度搜索、办公任务和推理基准上达到前沿性能表现。

Conclusion: MiniMax-M2系列验证了"小激活参数+大规模总参数量"的MoE架构在智能体场景下的有效性，且M2.7初步展现了自我进化的潜力，为未来更自主的智能体模型发展提供了方向。

Abstract: We introduce the MiniMax-M2 series, a family of Mixture-of-Experts language models built around the principle that mini activations can unleash maximum real-world intelligence. The flagship M2 contains 229.9B total parameters with only 9.8B activated per token. Designed end-to-end for agentic deployment, the M2 series rests on three components: (i) agent-driven data pipelines producing large-scale, verifiable trajectories across agentic coding and agentic cowork, each grounded in an executable workspace and an artifact-aligned reward; (ii) Forge, a scalable agent-native RL system that adapts to long-horizon agent trajectories, paired with windowed-FIFO scheduling, prefix-tree merging, inference optimization, and a clean training-inference-agent decoupling that supports both white-box and black-box agents; (iii) the latest M2.7 checkpoint takes an early step toward self-evolution -- autonomously debugging training runs and modifying its own scaffold. Across M2 through M2.7, this combination translates a mini-activation footprint into frontier-tier performance on agentic coding, deep search, office-task, and reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [173] [Which Changes Matter? Towards Trustworthy Legal AI via Relevance-Sensitive Evaluation and Solver-Grounded Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26530)
*Chen Linze,Cai Yufan,Hou Zhe,Dong Jin Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出法律推理应仅对法律相关变化敏感，构建了包含应变和不应变场景的统一评估套件，发现现有法律LLM存在系统性缺陷，并提出了基于形式推理的对抗性多智能体框架LexGuard来提升法律推理可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律推理需要区分"无关变化"和"法律相关变化"，但现有法律AI模型往往对无关扰动过度敏感，对关键法律要素却不够敏感，缺乏校准的敏感性评估方法。

Method: 提出LexGuard框架：将法律条文形式化为可执行约束，利用对抗智能体提取冲突的事实-法律论证，调用SMT求解器验证法律满足性和逻辑一致性。

Result: LexGuard有效降低了对操纵性框架的脆弱性，改善了相似法条间的区分能力，限制了无关法律属性的影响，并提升了良性改写下的一致性。

Conclusion: 法律可信赖性不仅需要准确性，还需要对法律实质性变化具有校准的敏感性。

Abstract: Legal reasoning requires distinguishing changes that matter from those that do not. Legal AI should remain stable under legally irrelevant perturbations, but should change when perturbations alter legally material points. We formulate this requirement as a legal-relevance-sensitive evaluation problem: LLMs should only be sensitive to the legally relevant change. We introduce a unified evaluation suite covering should-change and should-not-change evaluation across judicial fairness, robustness, and statute-confusion scenarios. Our evaluation shows that existing legal LLMs are systematically sensitive to legally irrelevant variations and often fail to distinguish related legal elements and statutory rules. To mitigate these failures, we present LexGuard, an adversarial multi-agent framework grounded in formal reasoning. LexGuard formalizes statutes into executable constraints, uses adversarial agents to extract competing fact-statute arguments, and invokes SMT solvers to verify legal satisfaction and logical consistency. Experiments show that LexGuard improves legal reasoning reliability by reducing vulnerability to manipulative framing, improving disambiguation among similar statutes, limiting the influence of legally irrelevant attributes, and increasing consistency under benign reformulations. We show that legal trustworthiness requires not only accuracy, but calibrated sensitivity to legally material changes.

</details>


### [174] [PolyFusionAgent: A Multimodal Foundation Model and Autonomous AI Assistant for Polymer Property Prediction and Inverse Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26543)
*Manpreet Kaur,Xingying Zhang,Qian Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PolyFusionAgent框架，结合多模态聚合物基础模型（PolyFusion）与工具增强、文献驱动的设计智能体（PolyAgent），实现聚合物发现中的预测、逆向设计与文献证据检索一体化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合物化学设计空间极其庞大，且结构、性质、先验知识呈现碎片化表征，导致AI模型脱离物理与实验现实，难以支持可直接操作的实验设计决策。

Method: 1. PolyFusion：多模态聚合物基础模型，对齐序列、拓扑、3D几何与指纹等不同视图，学习跨化学空间和数据体制的共享潜空间，改进热物理性质预测并支持性质条件生成。2. PolyAgent：工具增强的文献驱动设计智能体，通过检索聚合物文献中的证据，将预测和逆向设计链接起来，形成假设提出、评估与情境化的闭环。

Result: PolyFusion能够生成化学有效且结构新颖的聚合物，突破参考设计空间；PolyAgent实现设计闭环，为预测和逆向设计提供可验证的文献证据支持。

Conclusion: PolyFusionAgent实现了交互式、证据关联的聚合物发现，融合大规模表征学习、多模态化学知识与可验证的科学推理，弥合了AI模型与物理/实验现实之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Polymer discovery is central to fields ranging from energy storage to biomedicine, but it is hindered by an astronomically large chemical design space and fragmented representations of structure, properties, and prior knowledge. This fragmentation leaves many AI models disconnected from physical and experimental reality, restricting their ability to support directly actionable design decisions. Here we introduce PolyFusionAgent, an interactive framework coupling a multimodal polymer foundation model (PolyFusion) with a tool-augmented, literature-grounded design agent (PolyAgent). PolyFusion aligns complementary polymer views including sequence, topology, 3D geometry, and fingerprints across millions of polymers to learn a shared latent space transferable across chemistries and data regimes, improving thermophysical property prediction and enabling property-conditioned generation of chemically valid, structurally novel polymers beyond the reference design space. PolyAgent closes the design loop by linking prediction and inverse design with evidence retrieval from the polymer literature, proposing, evaluating, and contextualizing hypotheses with explicit precedent in one workflow. Together, PolyFusionAgent enables interactive, evidence-linked polymer discovery combining large-scale representation learning, multimodal chemical knowledge, and verifiable scientific reasoning.

</details>


### [175] [MobileExplorer: Accelerating On-Device Inference for Mobile GUI Agents via Online Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26546)
*Runxi Huang,Liyu Zhang,Shengzhong Liu,Xiaomin Ouyang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MobileExplorer 是一种通过在线探索加速移动 GUI 代理设备端推理的新框架，利用 VLM 推理间隙并行探索 UI 元素并记录为结构化记忆，配合两级回滚机制确保执行可靠性，将推理步骤和延迟降低 23%，任务成功率提升最高 5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动 GUI 代理多依赖云端推理，存在隐私隐患和网络延迟，完全设备端部署尚未充分探索。

Method: 利用 VLM 每步推理的长时间，并行轻量探索语义相关 UI 元素，记录探索轨迹为结构化记忆；设计两级回滚机制恢复初始 UI 状态；将探索轨迹总结为上下文提示注入后续推理步骤。

Result: 在 AndroidWorld 等基准上，平均推理步骤和端到端延迟降低 23%，任务成功率提升或保持最高 5%。

Conclusion: MobileExplorer 有效实现了全设备端移动 GUI 代理的低延迟、高成功率部署，平衡了隐私与性能。

Abstract: Mobile graphical user interface (GUI) agents enable AI models to autonomously operate smartphones on behalf of users. However, most existing systems focus primarily on optimizing task accuracy and rely on cloud-hosted models for inference, which introduces privacy concerns and network-dependent latency. As a result, fully on-device deployment of mobile GUI agents remains underexplored. We propose MobileExplorer, a new framework that accelerates on-device inference for vision-based mobile GUI agents via online exploration. The key idea is to exploit the long per-step reasoning time of vision-language models (VLMs) by performing lightweight, parallel exploration of UI elements. During model inference, the agent proactively probes semantically relevant UI elements and records these exploration traces as structured memory. To ensure reliable execution in live mobile environments, we design a two-level rollback mechanism that robustly restores the initial UI state when a fast but naive backtracking strategy fails. The collected exploration traces are then summarized into concise contextual hints and injected into the prompt to enhance the subsequent reasoning step. We evaluate MobileExplorer on multiple off-the-shelf devices using the AndroidWorld benchmark, as well as newly designed, more complex tasks and dynamic on-device environments. MobileExplorer reduces the average number of reasoning steps and end-to-end latency by 23\%, while maintaining or improving task success rates by up to 5\%. A video demonstration of MobileExplorer performance in the real world is available at https://youtu.be/thK7MJmdlvM .

</details>


### [176] [MedGuideX: Internalizing Decision Logic from Executable Guidelines into Large Language Models for Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26567)
*Yuhao Shen,Lang Cao,Simo Du,Yuqing Wang,Juexiao Zhou,Hao Peng,Yue Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于临床实践指南（CPG）的决策逻辑训练流程，通过将指南转化为可执行的临床决策逻辑，生成事实性和反事实性问答数据来训练医疗大语言模型MedGuideX，在四个临床推理基准上平均准确率提升10.28%，医生评估显示其推理步骤更符合临床实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将临床实践指南（CPG）作为自由文本训练数据或检索来源，未能充分利用其程序化决策结构，导致模型无法真正理解指南中的条件推理逻辑。

Method: 提出一条从指南派生的训练流程：将CPG建议转化为可执行的临床决策逻辑，基于此生成事实性和反事实性问答数据，使模型既学习指南支持的决策，也理解在不同患者条件下决策如何变化，最终微调得到MedGuideX。

Result: 在四个临床推理基准上，MedGuideX平均准确率相对提升10.28%；医生评估表明其能更好恢复临床医生的推理步骤，生成更忠实、有效、完整和清晰的推理理由。

Conclusion: CPG中的可执行决策逻辑可以转化为可扩展的监督信号，用于构建更可靠的医疗大语言模型。

Abstract: Clinical practice guidelines (CPGs) encode evidence-based decision logic that clinicians apply by evaluating patient variables, conditional criteria, and recommendation rules. However, existing methods often use CPGs as free-text training data or retrieval sources, underutilizing their procedural decision structure. To better exploit this structure, we introduce a guideline-derived training pipeline that transforms CPG recommendations into executable clinical decision logic and uses it to generate factual and counterfactual question-answering data. Theses data teach models both guideline-supported decisions and how decisions change under different patient conditions. Post-training a medical LLM on the generated data yields MedGuideX. Across four clinical reasoning benchmarks, MedGuideX achieves a 10.28% relative improvement in average accuracy. Physician evaluation further shows that MedGuideX better recovers clinician authored reasoning steps and produces physician-preferred rationales in faithfulness, validity, completeness, and clarity. Overall, our results show that executable decision logic from CPGs can be transformed into scalable supervision for building reliable medical LLMs.

</details>


### [177] [AGORA: Adapter-Grounded Observation-Action Retention for Inference-Free Prompt Compression in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26596)
*Haoran Zhang,Zhaohua Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对LLM Agent的token级压缩器存在失败模式"动作语法破坏"，本文提出AGORA——一种推理无关的步骤级压缩器，结合结构提示解析器、格式保留机制和125M参数的相关性评分器，在9个测试单元中8个保持≥75%的未压缩性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有token级抽取式压缩器在LLM Agent场景下失效（17个测试单元平均奖励≤0.05），原因在于压缩器会删除承载动作语义的低秩token（标识符、括号、动作动词），导致环境拒绝残差输出。

Method: 提出AGORA：1) 结构提示解析器进行步骤级压缩；2) 始终保留格式关键和近期关键内容的保底策略；3) 125M参数的相关性评分器，基于反事实下一动作变化标签训练（~2ms/步，零LLM开销）。

Result: 在9个测试单元中，AGORA是唯一在8个单元中保持≥75%未压缩性能的方法（唯一例外为73%）；四路组件消融实验表明结构保底策略是质量主导杠杆，学习型评分器在固定保留比率下实现1.0-11.5倍自适应端到端压缩。

Conclusion: token级压缩器不适用于Agent场景，步骤级压缩是正确方向；AGORA通过结构保底+轻量学习评分器实现高效压缩，无需额外LLM推理开销。

Abstract: The token-level extractive compressors widely used for general LM context are structurally inappropriate for LLM agents: across 17 (env, backbone, method) cells spanning two independent token-level method families, every cell collapses to mean reward <= 0.05 despite 1.3-13.3x realized compression. We name and characterize this failure mode as action-grammar destruction -- the tokens carrying action semantics (identifiers, brackets, action verbs) are exactly those self-information ranks lowest, so a general-purpose compressor reliably removes them and the environment rejects the residual. The diagnosis points to step-granularity compression. We introduce AGORA, an inference-free step-level compressor combining a structural prompt parser, an always-keep floor for format- and recency-critical content, and a 125M-parameter relevance scorer trained on counterfactual next-action-change labels (~2ms/step, zero per-step LLM toll). Across the compared inference-free and LLM-based methods, AGORA is the only one retaining >= 75% uncompressed performance in 8 of 9 cells (with the lone exception at 73%); a four-way component ablation isolates the structural floor as the dominant quality lever and the learned scorer as the source of 1.0-11.5x adaptive end-to-end compression from a single fixed keep ratio.

</details>


### [178] [FAST-GOAL: Fast and Efficient Global-local Object Alignment Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26615)
*Hyungyu Choi,Young Kyun Jang,Chanho Eom*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FAST-GOAL是一种高效的CLIP微调方法，通过全局-局部语义对齐增强模型处理长文本描述的能力，并引入GLIT100k数据集，在长/短描述数据集上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP等视觉-语言模型在预训练时使用简短摘要式描述，导致处理长文本描述时表现不佳，亟需一种高效方法使其适应详细文本描述。

Method: 提出两组件方法：1) FLISM通过目标检测和空间分割高效提取局部图像区域并与对应句子匹配；2) TSL最大化特定区域图像块token与对应区域嵌入的相似度。同时构建GLIT100k数据集提供全局-局部配对数据。

Result: 在长描述数据集(DOCCI, DCI)和短描述数据集(MSCOCO, Flickr30k)上均显著优于基线方法，计算效率高。

Conclusion: FAST-GOAL能高效地将CLIP适应于处理详细文本描述，同时保持计算效率和短描述能力。

Abstract: Vision-language models such as CLIP have shown impressive capabilities in aligning images and text, but they often struggle with lengthy and detailed text descriptions due to pre-training on short and concise captions. We present FAST-GOAL (Fast and Efficient Global-local Object Alignment Learning), an efficient fine-tuning method that enhances ability of CLIP to handle lengthy text through global-local semantic alignment. Our method consists of two key components. First, Fast Local Image-Sentence Matching (FLISM) efficiently extracts local image regions through object detection and spatial division, then matches them with corresponding sentences. Second, Token Similarity-based Learning (TSL) maximizes the similarity between patch tokens from specific regions in the image and their corresponding region embeddings, applying the same principle to text, which enhances the ability of the model to capture detailed correspondences. Additionally, we introduce GLIT100k, a dataset that provides both global image-lengthy caption pairs and context-derived local pairs, where local descriptions are extracted from global captions to maintain semantic coherence. Through extensive experiments on long caption datasets (DOCCI, DCI) and short caption datasets (MSCOCO, Flickr30k), we demonstrate that FAST-GOAL achieves significant improvements over baselines, enabling effective adaptation of CLIP to detailed textual descriptions while maintaining computational efficiency.

</details>


### [179] [Tail-Aware HiFloat4: W4A4 Post-Training Quantization for Wan2.2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26628)
*Zhanfeng Feng,Shuai Guo,Xin Di,Long Peng,Yang Cao,Zhengjun Zha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本报告提出Tail-Aware HiFloat4方法，通过适配ViDiT-Q后训练量化流程至Wan2.2，采用HiFloat4数值格式对主要线性层进行W4A4伪量化，同时保留敏感边界层高精度，并引入激活尾感知的百分位数校准模块构建通道掩码，有效减少校准异常值影响，保持运行时HiFloat4运算和采样流程不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对低比特文本到视频生成量化挑战，旨在降低模型量化过程中稀有校准异常值对性能的影响，同时保持HiFloat4算术和采样流程不变。

Method: 1. 适配ViDiT-Q后训练量化流程至Wan2.2；2. 采用HiFloat4数值格式对Wan2.2 transformer模块的主要线性层进行W4A4伪量化；3. 保留数值敏感的边界层高精度；4. 引入激活尾感知的百分位数校准模块用于通道掩码构建；5. 结合紧凑的PTQ状态恢复。

Result: 有效减少稀有校准异常值的影响，同时保持运行时HiFloat4算术和采样流程不变。

Conclusion: Tail-Aware HiFloat4在低比特文本到视频生成量化中实现了对异常值的鲁棒性，是一种实用的后训练量化方案。

Abstract: This report describes Tail-Aware HiFloat4, our submission to the low-bit text-to-video generation quantization challenge. Our method adapts the public ViDiT-Q post-training quantization pipeline to Wan2.2 under the HiFloat4 numerical format. We quantize the main linear layers in both Wan2.2 transformer modules with W4A4 HiFloat4 fake quantization, keep numerically sensitive boundary modules in high precision, and introduce an activation-tail-aware percentile calibration module for channel-mask construction. Together with compact PTQ-state restoration, this design reduces the influence of rare calibration outliers while keeping the runtime HiFloat4 arithmetic and sampling pipeline unchanged.

</details>


### [180] [UnityMAS-O: A General RL Optimization Framework for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26646)
*Yiqun Chen,Wei Yang,Erhan Zhang,Shijie Wang,Qi Liu,Zechun Niu,Bin Zhang,Haitao Li,Rui Li,Lingyong Yan,Jinyuan Feng,Biqing Qi,Xiaochi Wei,Yan Gao,Yi Wu,Yao Hu,Jiaxin Mao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: UnityMAS-O是一个将LLM多智能体工作流转化为可训练强化学习系统的通用框架，通过解耦逻辑角色与物理参数、支持多种参数共享模式、以及基于Ray的星型拓扑运行时，实现了对复杂多智能体工作流的统一RL优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM多智能体系统依赖人工编排（提示、工具、控制规则），缺乏统一的强化学习优化接口；现有RL后训练框架仅针对单策略优化，缺少对用户自定义多智能体工作流、结构化交互、角色信用分配和可配置参数共享的抽象支持。

Method: 提出UnityMAS-O框架：(1) 将完整工作流而非单响应作为优化单元；(2) 通过四个一级对象（逻辑智能体角色、图轨迹、用户定义奖励、智能体-模型映射）表示工作流；(3) 解耦逻辑智能体与物理模型参数，支持全共享、全分离、部分共享；(4) 基于Ray的星型拓扑运行时，中央控制器执行工作流、调用工具、记录轨迹、聚合奖励；模型本地工作组处理PPO-style分布式更新。

Result: 在检索增强QA（Natural Questions, HotpotQA）、迭代智能体搜索和反思性代码生成任务上，多智能体RL优化后改进了手动指定的工作流，小模型和严格代码全通过指标提升尤其显著。

Conclusion: UnityMAS-O可作为通用可复用基底，将多样化的LLM多智能体工作流转化为可训练的多智能体RL系统。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems decompose complex tasks into interacting roles, but most remain manually orchestrated by prompts, tools, and control rules, while agents are rarely optimized through a unified reinforcement learning interface. Existing RL post-training frameworks mainly target single-policy optimization and lack abstractions for user-defined multi-agent workflows, structured interaction, role-specific credit assignment, and configurable parameter sharing.
  We present UnityMAS-O, a general RL optimization framework for LLM-based multi-agent systems. UnityMAS-O treats the complete workflow as the optimization unit, rather than a single response or policy trajectory. It represents workflows through four first-class objects: logical agent roles, graph trajectories, user-defined rewards, and agent--model mappings. This decouples logical agents from physical model parameters, supporting full sharing, full separation, and partial sharing, with rewards assigned at role, turn, and trajectory levels.
  UnityMAS-O extends verl with a Ray-based star-topology runtime. A central controller executes workflows, invokes tools, records structured trajectories, and assembles rewards; model-local worker groups handle rollout, buffering, advantage computation, and distributed PPO-style updates. Users can define agents, workflows, model mappings, and rewards without rewriting the optimization infrastructure.
  We instantiate UnityMAS-O on retrieval-augmented QA, iterative agentic search, and reflective code generation. Across Natural Questions, HotpotQA, and held-out code tasks, multi-agent RL improves manually specified workflows after optimization, with especially large gains for smaller models and strict code all-passed metrics. These results show that UnityMAS-O can serve as a reusable substrate for converting diverse LLM-based multi-agent workflows into trainable multi-agent RL systems.

</details>


### [181] [Completion vs Optimality: Policy Gradient in Long-Horizon Cumulative-Damage Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26657)
*Wolfgang Maass,Sabine Janzen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有累积损伤的长期决策问题中策略梯度方法的两种失败模式，提出完成与最优性的分解方法，并通过砖匠和NBA球员两个校准环境验证了四个预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期决策问题中，局部吸引动作会导致全局不良后果，而策略梯度方法在此类问题上存在两种正交的失败模式，需要被识别和分离。

Method: 提出将问题分解为完成（到达终端时域）和最优性（匹配动态规划基准）两部分。在PPO框架下使用线性软惩罚，通过时域访问和动作空间限制两种干预手段进行实验。

Result: 仅提供时域访问会降低完成率；动作空间限制结合时域访问能实现完成但存在最优性差距（ΔM_final=0.271）。四个预测在砖匠（49步）和NBA球员（20赛季）两个环境中定性复现，时域不变性预测在H=15处出现边界一致例外。

Conclusion: 揭示了长期累积损伤问题中策略梯度方法失败的根本原因，分解框架为改进算法提供了理论基础，跨域复现验证了结果的泛化性。

Abstract: Long-horizon decision problems with cumulative damage couple locally attractive actions to globally adverse outcomes. We identify two orthogonal failure modes for policy-gradient methods on this class and propose a decomposition that separates them: \emph{completion} (reaching the terminal horizon rather than exiting via an implicit terminal constraint) and \emph{optimality} (matching the dynamic-programming reference given completion). Under PPO with a linear soft penalty, granting horizon access alone reduces the completion rate: the penalty's equilibrium drives the dominant-activity share to zero, while action-space restriction combined with horizon access achieves completion but leaves an optimality gap ($ΔM_{\text{final}} = 0.271$) that we trace to first-phase greedy commitment at the damage origin. We derive four testable predictions and evaluate them in two separately calibrated environments that share the same abstract structure but differ in domain, horizon, activity set, and calibration data: a 49-step bricklayer career and a 20-season NBA power-forward career. All four predictions replicate qualitatively. The horizon-invariance prediction is met at three of four tested horizons, with the exception at $H = 15$ consistent with the $H^*$ boundary ($H^* \in [6, 14]$ under the NBA parameters).

</details>


### [182] [MemFail: Stress-Testing Failure Modes of LLM Memory Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26667)
*Ishir Garg,Neel Kolhe,Dawn Song,Xuandong Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemFail是一个诊断基准，用于隔离和测试LLM记忆系统在摘要、存储和检索三个核心操作中的故障模式，通过对抗性数据集揭示不同记忆系统架构的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准将记忆系统视为黑箱，仅报告聚合问答准确率，无法将错误归因于具体故障模式，缺乏对记忆系统失败模式和设计选择的实证理解。

Method: 将记忆系统形式化为摘要、存储、检索三个规范操作，识别每个操作可能引发的故障模式，据此构建覆盖四个任务的五个对抗性数据集，评估四个SOTA记忆系统。

Result: 通过MemFail评估了四个最先进的记忆系统，展示了如何利用该基准实证理解不同记忆系统架构带来的权衡。

Conclusion: MemFail能够有效隔离和诊断LLM记忆系统的具体故障模式，为理解记忆系统架构设计选择提供实证依据。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on external memory systems to remain consistent across long-horizon interactions, but little empirical work has been done to understand the specific failure modes and design choices that these systems present. Existing benchmarks report aggregate question-answering accuracy and treat memory systems as black boxes, making it impossible to attribute an incorrect answer to a particular failure mode of the system. We introduce MemFail, a diagnostic benchmark that isolates the failure modes of modern LLM memory systems. We begin by formalizing memory systems as the composition of three canonical operations -- summarization, storage, and retrieval -- and identify the potential failure modes induced by each. Based on these hypothesized failure modes, we construct five datasets spanning four tasks, each adversarially designed to test a specific operation of a memory system. Using these datasets, we evaluate four state-of-the-art memory systems on MemFail and demonstrate how MemFail can be used to empirically understand the tradeoffs induced by differences in memory system architectures.

</details>


### [183] [Mind the Tool Failures: Achieving Synergistic Tool Gains for Medical Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26691)
*Yunhui Gan,Tan Pan,Kaiyu Guo,Limei Han,Weimiao Yu,Guangnan Ye,Chen Jiang,Yuan Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出在临床场景下，医疗AI代理工具可能失效，为此设计了一种基于GRPO强化学习框架的实例级工具选择方法，通过概率风险最小化、分歧感知协同学习和熵引导采样，显著提升了工具协同效果，在多个医疗基准上胜过基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗AI代理假设工具在其适用范围内可靠，但在真实临床中工具可能在困难实例上失败，导致不安全的下游决策。需要解决单个工具无法覆盖的失败实例，弥补最佳固定工具与理想实例级选择器之间的性能差距（Single-Oracle风险差距）。

Method: 提出基于GRPO的强化学习框架：(1) 概率风险最小化奖励；(2) 分歧感知协同学习奖励，促进对错误工具共识的实例级修正；(3) 熵引导采样策略，提升高分歧实例权重，从而更好地学习实例级工具协同。

Result: 在两个任务和七个医疗基准上，方法持续稳健地超越多个基线方法，验证了实例级工具协同选择对于可靠医疗代理系统的重要性。

Conclusion: 实例级工具选择比传统任务级工具选择更有效，分歧感知的协同学习与熵引导采样能够互补，显著缓解实例级异质性，提升医疗AI系统的安全性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Medical AI agents increasingly use external tools for diagnosis, treatment recommendation, and evidence retrieval, yet most existing approaches assume that task-appropriate tools are reliable within their intended scope. This assumption is fragile in real clinical settings, where even relevant tools may fail on challenging instances and lead to unsafe downstream decisions. To address this issue, we study medical tool use under imperfect-tool settings to correct failure instances missed by individual tools. Instance-dependent failure patterns create a gap between the best fixed single tool and an ideal instance-wise selector, which we refer to as the Single-Oracle risk gap. The core challenge is that conventional task-level tool selection cannot realize this gap, as it is inherently bounded by the performance of the best single tool. Motivated by this observation, we therefore account for instance-level heterogeneity and formulate tool use as an instance-level selection problem. Particularly, we propose a GRPO-based reinforcement learning framework with rewards for probabilistic risk minimization and disagreement-aware synergy learning, which promotes instance-level correction of erroneous tool consensus. Furthermore, an entropy-guided sampling strategy is adopted to upweight high-disagreement instances, which provide stronger signals for learning instance-specific tool synergy. These two components complement each other in mitigating instance-level heterogeneity and improving tool synergy. Experiments on two tasks and seven medical benchmarks show that our method consistently achieves robust and stable improvements over a broad range of baselines, highlighting the importance of synergy-aware tool use for reliable medical agentic systems.

</details>


### [184] [Towards Feedback-to-Plan Decisions for Self-Evolving LLM Agents in CUDA Kernel Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26720)
*Yee Hin Chong,Jiaming Wu,Youhui Zhang,Peng Qu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CUDAnalyst，通过轨迹冻结和选择性反馈注入方法，系统分析LLM作为自进化智能体生成CUDA内核时，规划决策对不同反馈信号的归因机制，发现显式规划仅在反馈对齐时有效，且结构化多反馈交互才能产生有效规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在CUDA内核生成中虽表现出色，但规划决策如何归因和组合异构反馈信号仍不透明，标准端到端消融实验无法解决此问题，因为迭代规划会放大早期扰动并将反馈效应与轨迹漂移混淆。

Method: 提出CUDAnalyst统一分析层，通过轨迹冻结（trajectory freezing）和选择性反馈注入（selective feedback injection）实现可控的、生成级别的规划决策归因，支持稳定的生成级评估和联盟式反馈效应归因。

Result: 显式规划仅在反馈对齐时有益；有效规划源于结构化多反馈交互；强推理模型的高级规划可部分迁移至弱模型。这些趋势在多个骨干模型、代表性工作负载和参考归纳机制下保持一致。

Conclusion: 论文揭示的反馈到规划的结构在可控分析维度内是鲁棒的，为理解LLM自进化智能体中的反馈-规划机制提供了系统性分析框架。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong empirical gains as self-evolving agents for CUDA kernel generation, driven by feedback-conditioned planning across generations. However, how planning decisions attribute and combine heterogeneous feedback signals remains opaque. Standard end-to-end ablations fail to resolve this question, as iterative planning amplifies early perturbations and conflates feedback effects with trajectory-dependent drift.
  We introduce \texttt{CUDAnalyst}, a unified analysis layer for controlled, generation-level attribution of planning decisions to feedback components via trajectory freezing and selective feedback injection. \texttt{CUDAnalyst} enables stable generation-level evaluation and principled coalitional-style attribution of feedback effects and interactions. Our results show that explicit planning is beneficial only when feedback is aligned, that effective planning emerges from structured multi-feedback interactions, and that high-level plans from stronger reasoning models can partially transfer to weaker ones. These trends hold across reference backbones, representative workloads, and reference induction regimes, indicating that the identified feedback-to-plan structure is robust within the controlled axes studied.

</details>


### [185] [It's Not the Capability: Harness Sensitivity Is Non-Monotone Across LLM Agent Tiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26731)
*Yong-eun Cho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过432次实验，测试了LLM代理中模型能力与最优约束程度呈单调反比关系的假设，发现该假设不成立：更强的约束可能降低前沿聊天模型性能，而推理模型在严格约束下表现最佳；不同能力层级的模型对约束的敏感性呈非单调关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"更强模型需要更少结构指导、结构化框架普遍提升可靠性"这一普遍假设，通过系统实验验证模型能力层级与最优约束复杂度之间的单调反比关系是否成立。

Method: 在HEAT-24基准上设计432次控制实验，跨越4个能力层级的6个模型，设置轻量、平衡、严格三种约束条件，并引入六标签失败分类法分析错误模式。

Result: 1) 前沿聊天模型(Gemini 2.5 Flash)中，增加约束使VTSR下降29-38个百分点，出现"约束复杂度悖论"；2) 前沿推理模型(Qwen3.5-122B)在严格约束下达到最高VTSR(91.7%)和最低延迟；3) 2B小模型(Gemma4:e2B)在所有约束下稳定达到91.7% VTSR。

Conclusion: 模型能力与最优约束之间不存在单调反比关系，约束敏感性取决于模型类型（聊天vs推理），并提出了实用的层级感知约束选择指南。

Abstract: A prevalent assumption in LLM agent deployment holds that more structured harnesses universally improve
  reliability, and that higher-capability models need proportionally less structural guidance -- together
  implying a monotone inverse relationship between model capability tier and optimal harness complexity. We
  test this hypothesis through a controlled 432-run experiment crossing six models across four capability
